Tauheed, Bab 9 «تجلی و مظہر»: hub plan

14 lessons from 5 Fasls. Every lesson opens and closes: tap a title. Inside, The steps is open; the rest is folded. Source refs are hidden; the Refs button shows them. Comment by selecting text, or with a section's button.

new taught here first · ↺ B3 S04 partly taught there; the new part is taught here · ↺ B1 S03 taught there; one-line reminder here · core repeat · ask kept in full, you decide · ask for you · Claude's judgement an import kept on Claude's framing

Gate D: for Ali to approve

Per-lesson Asks stay in each entry; Ali answers them on Reading Mode. The items below shape the plan.

  1. 14 lessons from 5 Fasls (the diagnosis table): U9.1 two (S01, S02), U9.2 three (S03 to S05), U9.14 four (S06 to S09), U9.15 three (S10 to S12), U9.16 two (S13, S14). Fold test (a fold where recalls empty a lesson): none is emptied; Bab 9's subject, manifestation and the Imams as His signs and channel, is mostly new (200 points taught here first). Fallback 12 (diagnosis): U9.2 two (S04 and S05 as one), U9.15 two (S11 as S10's last section). Recommended: 14.
  2. Size Asks the readers raised (each written as given): S07 (creative wilaya, 74 points and 77 re-read records) would split steps 6 and 7, the wonders, into a lesson of their own (S07 Ask 2; the U9.14-b diagnosis's own proposal); S12 might split refuge and al-Rida from the channel of grace (S12 Ask 44); S05 might split after step 6 (S05 load Ask). S11's reader found the load argues against folding it into S10 (S10 is already near 3,500 words). Recommended: split S07 into two (15 lessons), keep S05, S11 and S12 as drafted.
  3. Verdicts the readers changed after reading both passages (each an Ask in its entry): U9.1/26 SAME to core repeat (S02); U9.2/79 NONE to PART on B3 S06, U9.2/52 and /56 PART to core repeat (S04); U9.2/61 SAME to core repeat (S05); U9.14/45 SAME to PART (S06); U9.14/46 NONE to SAME on B3 S15, U9.14/29, /42, /47 NONE to PART (S07); U9.14/130 PART to SAME on B7 S11, RR4-029 PART to SAME on B4 S19 (S08); U9.15/50 NONE to PART, RR1-033 PART to SAME (S10); U9.15/15 NONE to core repeat, U9.16/11 NONE to PART, RR3-022 and RR3-023 NONE to SAME (S12). S13 found one repeat the judges missed (U9.16/2 in B4 S04's Teacher Copy) and kept NONE with an Ask. Recommended: accept.
  4. The AH re-read finding. 280 verified AH re-read records: 162 SAME (already taught in Bab 1 to 8), 51 PART, 67 NONE; 34 are off the Bab's subject (Imamat proofs, Mahdaviyat history and practice, the long life, the Imams' knowledge, the properties of names). As Bab 6 to 8 found, every Bab re-reads the same AH sessions, so most records are one recall line with the repeat under it as an Ask (BAB-LESSONS row 30, Bab 7). S07 holds 77 (49 SAME), S10 52. MM is absent from S07, S10 and S12; AH's new teaching there comes from the atlas points. Nothing was borrowed to fill it. Recommended: accept.
  5. Imports on Claude's judgement (thin lessons only: bottom quarter by AH/MM share, P25 33%: S01 15%, S08 16%, S09 25%, S02 32%). Sources, all later Babs: U13.33 (S01), U14.4 (S02), U10.14 (S08), U11.31 and U11.17 (S09). 90 rows judged: 9 kept (S01 1, S02 1, S08 4, S09 3), 81 dropped with the reason. Every kept row is Teacher Copy only with a CLAUDE'S JUDGEMENT (not the scholar's words) line; some kept texts still need the Jami check (named in S01, S08). Recommended: accept.
  6. Arabic printed again (55 texts across the Bab, each flagged with the earlier lesson; some only if a Teacher Copy item stays). Bab 7's lessons were written while this plan was drafted: the readers searched them too, and a vowel-free check found 68 distinct texts shared with Bab 7, 32 printed there in full (overlap/b7ar/arabic-table-b7.md). Bab 8 is being written (5 of 18 lessons on 2026-11-21). The readers set aside many table rows that share only a short run. Recommended: writers rerun the vowel-free search per lesson against Bab 7 and the written Bab 8 lessons (BAB-LESSONS 26).
  7. Rule 7. U9.1/4 (B6 S03 keeps it, Teacher Copy only) is taught here in full, flagged (S02). B1's 50 planned U9 imports were never written (ruling B1-A05); B4 dropped U9.2/20; B7 and B8 import nothing from Bab 9.
  8. Material held out of the hub (26 ledger points: U9.4, U9.5, U9.9, U9.10, U9.12, U9.13, U9.17 (M2 or later Fasls of this Bab), Maad) and the 34 off-subject re-read records: they stay in the Teacher Copy, flagged, not moved. Recommended: keep as held.
  9. Dependencies on Bab 8 (approved, being written): recalls that rest on Bab 8 Teacher Copy lines still under an Ask there (U9.2/47 and U9.14/17 on B8 S01's teacher note; several AH records on B8 S16 to S18). If Bab 8 drops such a line, the Bab 9 point is taught here instead (S04 Ask 8). Recommended: rule them with Bab 8's Asks.
  10. Sensitive AH remarks and weak texts found by the readers (each an Ask where it sits): tawassul claims and unsourced stories (the Siffin Nasibi's head, the Ma'ali al-Sibtayn report), Fatima as "qayyum", texts that are not a Masum's words («بيمنه رزق الورى», «بهم تحركت المتحركات», «تخلقوا»-type sayings), and late-only sources behind S06 step 3 («نحن مشية الله»). Recommended: Teacher Copy only, flagged, never Handout; print Jami's wording.
  11. Texts. 462 narration-like texts checked in Jami (FOUND 289, OTHER-WORDING 104, OTHER-SPEAKER 21, NOT-FOUND 34, POEM 14), each placed by lesson in open-questions/UNVERIFIED-TEXTS-B9.md (unmapped 0). The readers found misattributions that change print (S05, S08, S11, S13). The Arabic review (145 items) is on Reading Mode. Recommended: writers print Jami's wording with the Ask.
  12. The M2 and later Fasls of B09 (U9.3 to U9.13, U9.17 to U9.19) are not built. Recommended: later, after M1 is approved, as in Bab 5 to 8.
  13. Undertone: none, as Bab 5 to 8. Recommended: none.

New lessons from Bab 9 (for the orchestrator to add to BAB-LESSONS.md):

  • hub_shares.py rewrites HUB-LEDGER-REREAD.tsv from Fasl defaults unless hub.json carries rr_overrides: run it with the placement agent's homes as rr_overrides (or restore the placed file after it).

  • The shared-Arabic tables over-match on short runs (a stock phrase, one ayah inside a long matn) and miss texts carried by re-read records: readers corrected rows in most entries; the writers' own search stays required.
  • tf-idf shortlists miss real repeats; judges must grep the lessons' Arabic (from the B1 to B6 check).

The 14 lessons

اللہ اپنی آیات میں تجلی کرتا ہے مگر دیکھا نہیں جاتا؛ ہر مخلوق اس کا آئینہ ہے اور معصومین اس کی آیتِ عظمیٰ؛ وہ صادرِ اول اور مشیت کا ظرف ہیں، اس کے اذن سے ولایتِ تکوینی رکھتے ہیں اور اسے اپنے لیے استعمال نہیں کرتے؛ حجت عالم کی روح اور ہر فیض کا راستہ ہے؛ اسی لیے انہی کے وسیلے سے اس سے مانگا جاتا ہے۔

1. What tajalli is and is not S01 to S02

S01 اللہ اپنی مخلوق پر تجلی کرتا ہے مگر دیکھا نہیں جاتا، کیونکہ اس کی تجلی خود اس کی ذات کا ظاہر ہونا نہیں

ledger · 8 new · 14 part-taught before · 2 taught before · 0 kept in full
The steps 6
  1. What do the texts say about His tajalli? He "manifested to the mountain" (7:143, the base verse), "manifested to His creation without being seen, veiled by His light" (the Prophet's sermon), and a family of texts says the same: "by His creation", "in His Book", "to the intellects", the Simat (MM H1 P001:2-3, the clause only; JS H2 P017:8-10, P018:28-33 as one paragraph; U9.1/1, /14, /43; teacher note U9.1/44 from JS H2 P016). ↺ B1 S09 · B2 S08 · B3 S12 · B3 S03Already taught: B1 S09 "the same sermon of the Prophet, read there for sending the messengers" and B2 S08 "«المتجلي لخلقه بخلقه» and the other family texts already met (sermon 147 B3 S12, sermon 186 B3 S03)"New here: the sermon read as tajalli, 7:143 as base verse, the texts gathered as one family; U9.1/43 and /44 NEW
  2. Does manifesting mean being seen? No: what is manifest is not His Essence; Musa is told «لن ترانی»; Imam Rida knows Him «من غير رؤية» (MM H1 P006:7-8 cut to the claim; JS H4 P092:1, H3 P055, P082; EZ H3 P083 to P084 under MM H1 P001; U9.1/39). ↺ B3 S03 · B6 S01Already taught: B3 S03 "«متجل لا باستهلال رؤية»" and B6 S01/S02 "known without being seen; the Essence is not seen"New here: what is manifest in tajalli is not His Essence
  3. Why is the Nearest not seen? No creature veils Him; His only veil is His own light, the intensity of His manifestness (AH H1 P002:1-13; MM H1 P001:3; U9.1/2). ↺ B1 S06 · B4 S12Already taught: B1 S06 "AH: He is veiled by the ray of His light" and B4 S12 "creatures are not God"New here: no creature can veil Him, His veil is His own light
  4. What does the word mean? To appear and shine (jala); each thing appears by its own nature; a non-material thing shows only by an effect that is not itself (JS H2 P019:5-19 with 7:187 and 91:3, P020:6-23 Tabatabai's mutawa'a reported; EZ H2 P021:17-34 polisher, H4 P086:13-47 chocolate stain with smile and love once, H4 P096:4-13; U9.1/15, /45, /46, /50, and EZ's half of /40). new
  5. Which senses cannot be meant for God? Of the five senses (JS H2 P022:1-21), three are wrong: God Himself appearing or changing, or appearing after hiding like the sun from cloud (JS H2 P026, H4 P085:2-10; EZ H2 P031:1-12; MM H1 P004:7-11 carrier); the cause descending into its effect (JS H2 P032:1-14, P034:2-11); self-transformation, wax or sea and waves, and its form "a ray thinning into a raqiqa" (JS H2 P035:2-13, P037:2-29); so the Ibn Arabi reading is refuted plainly (MM H1 P006:3 carrier) and a jilwa is never God Himself (EZ H4 P097, H2 P031:7-9) (U9.1/16 to /21, JS's half of /40, /51). ↺ B2 S08 · B5 S10 · B4 S11 · B4 S12 · B4 S10 · B6 S03 · B7 S14Already taught: B2 S08 "JS: tajalli has several senses", B5 S10 "no change or motion in God", B4 S11 "nothing comes out of Him", B4 S12 "creatures are not God, the mystics' 'nothing but Himself' is an error", B4 S10 "the world as God and His manifestations is not right", B6 S03 "He is not sensed as the made is", B7 S14 "not an agent by self-manifestation or outpouring"New here: the full list of five, and each wrong sense refuted in tajalli terms in JS's and EZ's wording; U9.1/51 NEW, TC only
  6. Then in what sense does He manifest? Lead-in to S02 (one {+} paragraph, JS H3 P059:4 as the scholar line). [NEW, bridge]

Who carries it

MM steps 1 and 2 (the Prophet's sermon, the Rida hadith), AH and MM step 3 (AH carries it). Steps 4 and 5, the lesson's core analysis, are JS's alone, with EZ's examples; AH/MM are absent there except MM's carrier lines (H1 P004:7-11, P006:3). Both AH re-read records are SAME recalls, so the new AH teaching here is only H1 P002 (thin, as BAB-LESSONS row 30 expects). One import (U13.33/3) brings AH and MM onto step 2 in the Teacher Copy only.

AH re-read adds

RR3-017 (AZ-S29 ¶209-222, Imam drowned in light, parallel to step 3), SAME, recalled; RR3-039 (ZH-S06 ¶164-168, Fatima's noble face, step 2), SAME, recalled. No new AH teaching from the re-read.

Imports 2
  • Kept: U13.33/3 (Liqa' Allah is finding, not seeing by eye or form; MM, AH), Teacher Copy only, under step 2, never its ground: AH SE-S07 ¶40 (U13.33 P014:3-4, God has no sensible, imaginal or intellectual form, so meeting Him is not seeing a form), AH SE-S03 ¶92 (P015:8, He has no form, no shape, is not a body), MM MMB_07_S05 ¶223 (P013:1-3, meeting Him is not by the senses; Rida «عُرف بغير رؤية»). Claude's judgement if even the meeting the Quran promises is no sight of a form, then His manifesting cannot be His being seen, for He has no form to be seen. AH's «خدا نور نیست» (P015:8) is read beside step 3: His veil of light is no sensible light.
  • Dropped: no sound framing (each supports another subject, would only add a voice): U13.33/2 (meeting as the Quran's greatest glad tiding, degrees: liqa' subject, not this step; MM's Abu Basir line on the heart's seeing belongs to the heart sense, S02); U13.33/4 (inner finding when means are cut: fitra, S02's sense); U13.33/5 (finding Him by His manifestation without signs or reason: the second right sense, S02's step, not "not seen"); U13.33/6 (finding grows with obedience); U13.33/7 (bewilderment grows); U13.33/8 (people differ in witnessing); U13.33/9 (the Messenger sent to bring people to meeting); U13.33/10 (the prophets' annihilation in remembrance as proof of mission); U13.33/14 (wijdan as a term); U13.33/15 (hardship clears the fitra); U13.33/16 (self-sufficiency cuts from God, the scorpion charm); U13.33/17 (prophets' inner powers withheld); U13.33/18 (contentment at Ashura); U13.33/20 (18:110 for the Prophet's distinction); U13.33/21 (a long-parted friend); U13.33/22 (the Throne of knowledge and glad tidings); U13.33/23 (illumination by the Throne's light, ijtiba'); U13.33/24 (opponents' flight from remembrance, witness by obedience); U13.33/28 (the lover and the night watchman); U13.33/29 ("give me one day and withhold one day").

پھر وہ کس معنی میں تجلی کرتا ہے؟ اپنی آیات سے، اور دل پر (S02)

S02 «خدا کی تجلی اس کی ذات کا ظاہر ہونا نہیں: وہ اپنی آیات کے ذریعے اور دل کی فطری پہچان میں جلوہ گر ہے»

ledger · 1 new · 20 part-taught before · 5 taught before · 1 kept in full
The steps 7
  1. What is the first right sense? He brings into being things that are His signs; the made shows its Maker, and showing signs is His tajalli, not the Essence appearing. MM H1 P001:8 «دليله آياته» as the AH/MM line; JS H2 P022:24 (3:190), P038:2-4, P039:11-12, P045:2-5, P046:2-8; EZ H2 P041:4-11, P048:10-12 (one artisan example); the student's conclusion H2 P051 told without the classroom frame. (U9.1/22, /23, /24, /25, /34; RR3-002, RR3-018, RR4-034 under the recall.) ↺ B1 S05 · B3 S01 · B7 S03 · B6 S05Already taught: B1 S05 "creatures are signs that show His power; whatever one looks at is a sign; the made shows its Maker" (also B3 S01, S04, S08, S13; B7 S03), and B6 S05 "God showed the Prophet His signs, not His Essence",New here: JS's claim that creatures are His tajalli exactly in this sense, that the tajalli texts mostly mean the signs, and the general rule that tajalli is His showing signs, in P038:2-4, P039:11-12, P045:2-5, P046:2-8, P051, P022:24
  2. Where do the texts say it? Four sermons, one text and one inference each: by His creation (sermon 108, JS H3 P061:3-8, with P065:4-8 "no new route of proof"); in His Book "by showing His power" (sermon 147, JS H3 P070:6, P069:3-5; EZ H3 P075:9 one line on reading the Quran); to the intellects (sermon 186, JS H3 P077:3-11) and to the faculties "by them, and by them He is beyond them" (sermon 185, JS H3 P079:5, P080:2-4, with «لم تحط به الأوهام»); to the eyes (sermon 165, JS H4 P095:3-16, the a fortiori: a creature He made manifest to eyes cannot be described, how then the Essence). (U9.1/33, /48, /35, /36, /37, /38, /49.) ↺ B3 S12 · B3 S03 · B3 S09Already taught: B3 S12 "sermon 147, His tajalli found inwardly in the Quran", B3 S03 "by things He is manifest to intellects and by them veiled", B3 S09 "the peacock sermon for His power in creation",New here: «بما أراهم من قدرته» (P070:6), Ali's «وبها امتنع منها» (P079:5, P080:2-4), «جلاه» as evidence for the sense of tajalli (P095:3-16). SAME: recall only for sermon 108 (B2 S08), sermon 186's «بها تجلى صانعها للعقول» (B3 S03), the Quran as jilwa (B3 S12) and "by His creation is no new route" (B2 S08)
  3. If creation shows Him, how is it also His veil? Both reports are true: by the things He is manifest to intellects and by them withheld from eyes, and the wording differs with the hearer: some are told He is self-disclosed, some that He is veiled by His light. AH H1 P014:5-8, H4 P088:1-22; JS H3 P077:3, P077:15-21. (U9.1/12, /42.) ↺ B1 S05 · B1 S06 · B1 S09Already taught: B1 S05 "His creation is a veil between Him and them (JS)" and B1 S06 "veiled by the ray of His light (AH)" (also B1 S09),New here: AH's claim that both statements are true together (P014:5-8) and his rule that reports differ by hearer (P088:1-22). The joining of P014 with P077:3 is the lesson's own reconciliation: ASK
  4. What is the second right sense? The fitri knowledge He makes known: unacquired, proclaimed to all, His Essence being manifestation itself; above proof, found by the heart outside the two limits of ta'til and tashbih, surfacing in distress and growing with purification; JS accepts the three levels (acts, attributes, Essence) only as this heart-knowledge. AH H1 P005:1-13, P012:1-10 carry it; MM H1 P004:1-2 (light of knowledge, lost when intellected), H4 P087:37 («ليس على الناس أن يعلموا حتى يكون الله هو المعلم لهم»); JS H2 P052:5-8, H3 P053:6-11, P054:1-8, P055:5-12, P056 (TC). (U9.1/4, /10, /26, /27, /28, /29, /30.) ↺ B2 S01 · B2 S07 · B3 S14 · B2 S08 · B1 S06 · B6 S05Already taught: B2 S01 "God known to all by fitrah, evident to the inner self", B2 S07 and S15 "knowledge of God is not acquired", B3 S14 "hidden by the intensity of its manifestation", B2 S08 "the heart's testimony is stronger than argument; tajalli has several senses, the right one the heart's light", B1 S06 "leaving the two limits of ta'til and tashbih", B6 S05 "heart-sight without the eye",New here: AH's tajalli and kasb exclude each other and His Essence is manifestation itself (P005, P012), MM's light-of-knowledge image (P004:1-2), JS's joining of heart and reason under affirmation without likening (P053:6-11), the heart finding Him there (P054:1-8), the three levels (P056, TC). Core repeat kept in full: U9.1/26 (P052:5-8). Rule 7: U9.1/4 taught here in full
  5. Why is it forgotten, and who brings it back? Heedlessness veils it; the prophets are sent to claim the covenant of fitrah, and the Imams remind (tadhkir) of the One already known. AH H1 P015:1-5, P015:19 (with «ليستأدوهم ميثاق فطرته», P015:5); MM H4 P087:41 (one line). (U9.1/13.) ↺ B1 S11 · B2 S06Already taught: B1 S11 "by us God was known (MM)" and B2 S06 "the Imams' words remind of the fitri God",New here: AH's tadhkir framing (P015:1-5) and «وأنت الكتاب المبين», an ascribed verse, only as «منسوب» if kept (ASK)
  6. Are lights seen in worship, or what the friends of God are shown, a sight of God? No: He discloses His greatness, not His Essence; such lights and forms are created signs, by which He raises degrees of certainty. MM H1 P003:1 («إنما يكشف عن عظمته ويري أولياءه نفسه حيث شاء»), P006:5 (the one Mi'raj line, recall); JS H3 P057:12-22, P058 (the Najaf story, ASK), H4 P093:2-7. (U9.1/3, /5, /31, /47.) ↺ B5 S10 · B1 S14 · B3 S12Already taught: B5 S10 "the descent dialogue: descent is not motion; He discloses His greatness" and B1 S14 "certainty has degrees", B3 S12 "extraordinary events point to God",New here: the report read as tajalli to His friends (P003:1), and JS's lights in worship as created signs raising certainty (P057:12-22, P093:2-7). NEW: U9.1/31. SAME: recall only for the Mi'raj line (B6 S05/S06)
  7. Close: what tajalli can never mean, and the two right senses. No appearance after hiding, no change, no descent, no eye-vision, no fana or hulul; He manifests by His signs and on the heart. JS H3 P059:4-5, P060:16-19. (U9.1/32.) ↺ B6 S03 · B5 S10 · B5 S03Already taught: B6 S03 "no eye-vision, no change", B5 S10 "no descent", B5 S03 "no indwelling", each taught alone,New here: gathering them as the limits of tajalli, and fana (P059:4-5, P060:16-19)

Who carries it

JS carries the lesson (steps 1, 2, 4, 6, 7 are his analysis); AH carries step 3 (P014, P088) and the core of step 4 (P005, P012) and step 5 (P015); MM gives single lines (P001:8 in step 1, P004:1-2 and P087:37 in step 4, P003:1 and P006:5 in step 6, P087:41 in step 5). AH/MM are absent from steps 2 and 7 and from the sense analysis of step 1. This is a thin lesson (AH/MM share 32%, bottom quarter, cut-off 33%); most AH re-read records here are recalls, so the new AH/MM teaching stays thin, as BAB-LESSONS row 30 expects. EZ adds the artisan example (step 1) and one line on reading the Quran (step 2).

Next lesson picks up

«اگر ہر مخلوق اس کی آیت ہے تو خدا اپنی آیات اور آئینوں میں خود کو کیوں اور کیسے دکھاتا ہے، اور کیا سب آیات ایک درجے کی ہیں؟» (If every creature is His sign, why and how does God show Himself in His signs and mirrors, and are all signs of one degree?) (S03)

2. His signs, mirrors and the greatest sign S03 to S05

S03 یہ سارا عالم خدا کا آئینہ، اس کی آیت اور اس کا کلمہ ہے؛ انسان ان میں خاص آئینہ ہے جو اپنی قدرت و اختیار سے بھی اور اپنی محتاجی سے بھی اسے دکھاتا ہے؛ آئینے درجوں میں مختلف ہیں، مگر ہر زمانے میں ایک قد نما آئینہ رہتا ہے: سب سے پہلے رسول اللہ صلی اللہ علیہ و آلہ، اور آج امام حسن عسکری علیہ السلام کے فرزند

ledger H03 · 13 new · 19 part-taught before · 1 taught before · 0 kept in full
The steps 8
  1. Why does God make Himself known by signs at all? His Essence is beyond every thought (AH P014:1, in Urdu; the verse line as «منسوب», Q25) and beyond man's understanding, yet man needs to know Him for his felicity, so He shows Himself in His signs, 41:53 (EZ U9.2-2 P051:23-24, block once, printed again flagged). So the prophet is not only for rulings: things are the signs of His majesty, and knowing their reality is knowing His perfections, a journey with no end (AH U9.2-1 P003:1-8; U9.2/2). Recap of S01 and S02 in one line: the signs describe Him without making Him described (AH P004:2, P004:5). Hence the existent realities are His marks, signs, attributes and stations, as the Rajab tawqi' of the Imam of the Age says: «فجعلتهم معادن لكلماتك وأركانا لتوحيدك وآياتك ومقاماتك ... يعرفك بها من عرفك» (AH P004:3, which alludes to it, with the text from EZ U9.2-4 P120:10-11; printed in full here, S04 recalls it; U9.2/3). [U9.2/2 PART: recall B1 S01 "the prophet's chief teaching is tawhid, not rulings" then new: the realities he teaches are His signs, so knowing things is knowing Him, in AH P003:3-8. U9.2/3 PART: recall B1 S05 "He is known by signs and established by marks" then new: AH's reading of the tawqi', the realities as His marks, signs and stations, in AH P004:3-5 with the tawqi' text. The «انفعال، وجدان اور مالکیت» sentence (P004:1, P004:4, unexplained) TC only, flagged (pointer to U9.13)]
  2. What is a sign, and what is the world to one who can read? A book of His signs: accidents and substances its letters, their meanings its verses (AH U9.2-6 P149:1-3). The sun encompasses the earth and holds it by its pull, and the earth cannot contain it; so the sun of divinity has shone on the quiddities of the possible and given them being, «الله نور السماوات والأرض» (AH P146:1-17, 24:35 opening, printed again flagged). Every particle is a niche for the light of the unseen and glorifies Him: «يسبح لله ما في السماوات وما في الأرض» says «ما», not «من» (AH P148:2-7, 62:1). Before creation there was no mirror before His face; then He showed a face to every mirror, small and great (AH P150:2-6, the book named, the poet's name out of the Handout). «Word» names the same thing: kalima is what shows the hidden, as the bone shows white under the knife (kalama al-'azm), so every thing that wears existence is a word of God (EZ U9.2-2 P033:7-22, P033:74-82, one telling); and the Quran calls a prophet's proof «آیت», not «معجزہ», because like light it is manifest in itself and manifests what is beyond it (JS P038:6-16, EZ P039:3-10). One-line bridge: mirror, sign and word are three names for one thing (Q2). [U9.2/70 PART: recall B1 S05 "God is in things without being contained, as light encompasses the bulb" then new: the sun holding the earth by gravity, read for the sun of divinity over the quiddities, in AH P146:12-17. U9.2/72 PART: recall B3 S03 "every particle points to God and cries 'God'" then new: each particle a niche for the light of the unseen and «ما» in 62:1, in AH P148:2-7. U9.2/73 PART: recall B1 S05 "the world is a book whose lines are the creatures" then new: «no mirror before His face», then a face shown to every mirror, in AH P150:2-6. U9.2/21 PART, first half: recall B3 S03 "every creature is a sign" and B8 S02 "creatures as His words, kalima shows the hidden" then new: EZ's word read as the same thing as AH's mirror (the highest words at step 6). U9.2/23 PART: recall B1 S08 "light is manifest in itself and manifesting others" then new: ayah as the Quran's own name for a proof, tied to tajalli, in JS P038:14-16 and EZ P039:3-10. AH's world as a book (RR3-003), the tongue of law and of creation (RR3-001), the lamp drowned in its light (RR4-004) and the soul as His mathal (RR4-003, TC) are SAME and sit under the B1 S05 recalls; the proofs as His creational word joined without break (RR1-008, TC) is SAME under the B1 S10 recall]
  3. Is man a sign in a special way? Yes. God gave His servants of His own power and choice so they would know their Lord's power and choice and be mirrors of the Able and Choosing; so prophecy, religion, obedience and sin rest on choice and trial, not compulsion (AH U9.2-1 P001:4-8, P002:1-2); the sun has power but no choice: told to shine, could it refuse? (EZ U9.2-4 P104:17-25, one line). Man is also the sign of creating, in his own realm: he pictures a garden ten farsakhs wide and it exists in his soul as long as he attends to it, as things exist by His «کن» (AH P005:4-14, 36:82; the saying that the gnostic creates by his himma given in Urdu, the philosopher and book unnamed, Q25). And God's successor: «إني جاعل في الأرض خليفة» meant to make man His successor, mirror, niche and locus on earth (AH P006:3-4, first half of 2:30 only, Q5); as His vicegerent man can rise above his drives (EZ U9.2-4 P105:15-19, one line, no names, Q15). [U9.2/1 PART: recall B1 S06 "infer His perfect power from the power He gave us" then new: power and choice given so that man mirrors the Able and Choosing, hence trial and religion, in AH P001:4-8, P002:1-2. U9.2/42 PART: recall B1 S06 (and B7 S11, S14: qadir mukhtar, our free choice His sign) then new: man named the mazhar of al-Qadir al-Mukhtar in the tajalli frame, EZ's sun, P104:19-25, one line under AH. U9.2/4 NEW. U9.2/5 PART: recall B1 S11 "the khalifa is a sign for knowing God" then new: AH on 2:30, man His mirror and locus on earth, in AH P006:3-4. U9.2/43 NEW. AH's «each of you is an ayatullah al-uzma, have you understood yourself?» (RR4-005) is SAME under the B3 S03 recall; Ali's verse on the greater world folded in man (RR5-055, TC) is SAME under the B1 S05 recall]
  4. Is then every human, and everything a human does, His sign? A successor must carry a sample of the one he succeeds: the farrier's successor is not a watchmaker (AH U9.2-6 P147:3-7; the saying «إن الصورة الإنسانية هي أكبر حجج الله على خلقه» TC only as «منسوب», Q8 of this entry). As a merchant cannot bring his whole warehouse into the shop and sets out one sample of each kind, so the perfections lie in His unseen treasuries, «وإن من شيء إلا عندنا خزائنه وما ننزله إلا بقدر معلوم», and the sample in the shop of this world is the Prophet, Ali and the Baqiyyat Allah (AH P151:1-12, P152:1-12, 15:21). But not every human: the humanity of Shimr and his like was a sign, and they ruined it themselves (EZ U9.2-8 P177:1-10, 41:53 inline as the opener). And our being is His word by creation, not our words and deeds, which often lead away from Him: the flower's being is His word, its lie would not be; so the creature called «word of God» is not His speech (EZ U9.2-2 P037:10-21, JS P036:3-12). [U9.2/71 PART, TC: recall B3 S09 "man is God's greatest creational sign" then new: the human form saying, «منسوب», in AH P147:1-2. U9.2/74 PART: recall B1 S05 "God has no likeness but this world holds samples, a branch brought from the garden" (and B7 S02, S03: AH's samples from His storehouse brought to the shop of this world) then new: the merchant's warehouse read with 15:21, and the sample named as the Prophet, Ali and the Baqiyyat Allah, in AH P151:4-12, P152:6-12; close to a repeat, Q11. U9.2/91 NEW. U9.2/22 PART: recall B8 S02 "a creature is called His word because it shows the hidden" then new: the creature as His word is not His speech, and our deeds are not His words, in EZ P037:10-21 and JS P036:3-12. AH's mithl and mathal, samples of His Essence and attributes placed in man (RR4-001), and man honoured for them (RR4-002) are SAME under the B1 S05 recall]
  5. How does a servant show his Lord? By need. «Teach me» makes the servant's ignorance the mirror of His knowledge; «I am a beggar, give» shows His mastery; «I am mean, You are generous» shows His generosity (AH U9.2-1 P015:1-6, P015:13-19, with the munajat line in Jami's wording, Q7); this is the servant's perfection and nearness, his being becoming the mirror of the Worshipped (P015:8-12; «العبودية جوهرة كنهها الربوبية» TC only pending its source, Q7). The beggar who stretches his hand to the rich merchant makes his wealth plain, as the Khorasan billionaire in plain clothes was known only by the beggars who came to him (AH U9.2-7 P157:4-40, one telling); so at His door our «give», «I am weak», «I do not know» make us mirrors of His wealth, power and knowledge (P157:51-65, P158:23-24). And «there is no repetition in self-disclosure»: every supplication, qunut and prayer of the Mafatih answers one particular manifestation, and only the perfect man knows them all (AH U9.2-6 P155:1-17, P156:11-16; the maxim in Urdu, not as a text; Du'a al-Sabah's opening once, P155:18). [U9.2/12 NEW (the jewel line held back pending its source). U9.2/78 PART: recall B1 S06 "the mirror of one's poverty shows His richness; the ignorant man's question and the beaten weak man are mirrors of knowledge and power" then new: only the beggar and the rich merchant telling, P157:4-40; the ignorant and weak examples and the conclusion (P157:41-50, P158) are the same as B1 S06 and sit under the recall with RR4-007 and RR4-008, Q12. U9.2/77 PART: recall B7 S20 "His disclosure has no bound" (also B8 S14, S17: each manifestation its own need and address) then new: no repetition in tajalli, each supplication answering one manifestation, in AH P155:5-14, merged with H1's «ناز و نیاز». U9.2/83 NEW, TC: «اللهم إن هذا منك وبك ولك وإليك» (AH P165:1-7), the servant's lack shows the Lord, Q13. The lack as the mirror of perfection (RR4-007), our beggary showing His Godhood (RR4-008), Ali fainting before His qahhariyya (RR4-010, TC) and Imam Hasan's many kinds of worship as a varied mirror (RR4-011, TC) are SAME under the B1 S06, B1 S17 and B1 S18 recalls]
  6. Are all signs equal? No: all are His mirrors, but mirrors differ: the pocket mirror shows the brow, a larger one head and neck, the full-length mirror head to toe; so the scholars show a needle's point of His knowledge, the strong His power, the awliya more, the prophets more still (AH U9.2-1 P007:1-6, P007:13-14, P007:25-31, P008:4-19; the controlling image). One line each from EZ: bulbs of two hundred volts and a projector bulb on one generator (U9.2-4 P110:16-17); the higher your knowledge, the greater a sign you are (P115:8-10; «سلونی» to S04). Words too: God has no idle talk, but among His words are good and better, and the highest words are the Prophet and the Imams (EZ U9.2-2 P034:3-20, P035:8-13; «نحن كلمات الله العليا» TC only in Jami's nearest wording, Q10). [U9.2/6 PART: recall B3 S04 "signs differ in degree" (also B8 S01) then new: AH's mirror image, pocket, beard and full-length, in AH P007, P008. U9.2/48 PART: recall B3 S04 then new: lamps on one generator, EZ P110:17, one line. U9.2/50 NEW. U9.2/21 PART, second half: recall B3 S04 "the Infallibles are His greatest sign" then new: the highest words are the Prophet and the Imams, in EZ P034:16-20, P035:8-13]
  7. Objection: can God's splendour be more and less? Du'a al-Sahar asks «اللهم إني أسألك من بهائك بأبهاه وكل بهائك بهي». His splendour is one perfect splendour; the comparative belongs to its manifestations, which differ as signs differ, so «بأبهاه» means: by that manifestation of Your splendour which shows it most; and «كل بهائك بهي», «كل جلالك جليل»: even the lesser manifestation is perfect, without defect (JS U9.2-2 P040:5-31, P040:32-33, P043:1-16, the simpler reading, no "other views" label). EZ adds that the second splendour, majesty and light, by which one asks, is the luminous being of the Seal of the Prophets (P041:31 block, P041:49-55, P042:9-14). [U9.2/24 NEW. U9.2/25 NEW. EZ's owning the light without being walis (P041:4-19) held for U9.13, TC pointer]
  8. Is there one mirror that shows Him whole? Yes. For the rational, able and choosing human (not for animals and stones) there must always be in this world a full-length mirror of God, knowing, able, kind and merciful, showing each perfection of His majesty and beauty as far as the capacity of the possible allows; each vessel takes by its capacity, as a tank or a bottle breaks past its measure (AH U9.2-1 P009:1-5, P007:29-31; 13:17 out of the Handout, Q6). The first and greatest is the Prophet: the full-length mirror of all the names of beauty and majesty, «ayatullah al-uzma» (AH P013:1, P014:2-17), God's most manifest locus (P011:1, TC with the verse of the falcon of the placeless, P011:3-5); having no like, he mirrors the God who has no like: His ahadiyya needed a mirror (AH U9.2-6 P153:5-9; the verse «Ahad became manifest in the mim of Ahmad», TC, «منسوب», P154:1-4, Q14). And today that mirror is the son of Imam Hasan al-Askari, known by certain transmission; every claimant in the Major Occultation was disgraced (AH U9.2-1 P010:1-4, placed after the Prophet, the built order reversed). The necessity proof (P017 to P022) goes to S10; the Prophet's power over things (P013:2-9) to S07 as a pointer. [U9.2/7 NEW. U9.2/11 PART: recall B3 S04 "the Infallibles are His greatest sign absolutely" then new: the Prophet as the full-length mirror of all His names of jamal and jalal, in AH P013:1, P014:11-17. U9.2/9 NEW, TC. U9.2/75 NEW. U9.2/76 NEW, TC. U9.2/8 PART: recall B1 S03 "AH calls the Mahdi a mirror of Ali's universal wilaya" then new: the Imam of the Age as today's full mirror of God, in AH P010:1-4. The perfect man as the mirror of all perfections by every kind of begging (RR4-009, TC) and the taraddud as jamal and jalal, fullest in the perfect man (RR4-033, TC) are SAME under the B1 S11 and B1 S06 recalls] This hands S04 the question: after the Prophet, who is the greatest sign?

Who carries it

AH carries steps 1, 2 (book, sun, particle, mirror), 3, 5, 6 and 8 (U9.2-1 H1 whole, U9.2-6 and U9.2-7 lines). EZ carries the word half of step 2 and step 4's second half (Shimr; being, not deeds), adds one line each in steps 3, 6 and 7; JS carries step 7 and adds in steps 2 and 4. MM is absent from all of U9.2 (diag Q1). All 15 AH re-read records placed here are SAME recalls of Bab 1 to 6: they add no new AH teaching; the lesson's new AH teaching is all in ledger points (BAB-LESSONS row 30). Next lesson picks up

رسول اللہ صلی اللہ علیہ و آلہ کے بعد خدا کی سب سے بڑی آیت کون ہے، اور کسی آیت کے «سب سے بڑی» ہونے کا مطلب کیا ہے؟ (After the Prophet, who is God's greatest sign, and what does it mean for a sign to be the greatest?) (S04)

S04 «حجتِ خدا سب سے بڑی آیت ہے: پیغمبر صلی اللہ علیہ و آلہ کے بعد علی علیہ السلام اور معصومین علیہم السلام خدا کی آیتِ عظمیٰ ہیں، کیونکہ کاریگر کا بہترین کام اسے سب سے زیادہ دکھاتا ہے اور وہ خدا کو بیان بھی کرتے ہیں؛ اور فاطمہ سلام اللہ علیہا وہ آیتِ کبریٰ ہیں جن کی گواہی خود خدا دیتا ہے»

ledger H04 · 14 new · 12 part-taught before · 3 taught before · 4 kept in full
The steps 6
  1. What sets a sign of God apart, and what is a hujja? In everything He has a sign, but a hujja is the decisive sign; prophets and awliya are set apart by God-given knowledge and power, which the Quran calls a clear sign (آیہ بیّنہ), not by miracles as such; miracles are a sign, but the Imams did not build on them (EZ P109, P110, P118 one line; EZ P122:7-11; EZ P123:1-2; U9.2/47, U9.2/51, U9.2/54, U9.2/55). [U9.2/47 SAME: recall B8 S01 "hujja is the decisive sign among sign-words" (base B1 S05, B3 S03) | U9.2/51 SAME: recall B3 S04 "the title Ayat Allah al-Uzma is relative for scholars, absolute for the Infallibles" | U9.2/54, U9.2/55 NEW]
  2. Who is the greatest sign? The awliya's very being is His sign; the absolute Ayat Allah al-Uzma is the Infallibles: the Prophet first, then Ali, the Prophet's self and his full-length mirror; stated once as a reconciliation (U9.2-a Q3). In names: man is God's great name, prophets and awliya His greatest name, Ali the greatest-greatest (JS P044:8-12, P046:4-12, P050:15-20; EZ P113:2-7, P111:7-11, P114:1-3, P120:12-13; EZ P116 one salam «السلام عليك يا آية الله العظمى»; AH U9.14-2 P018:1-5, 34-36; RR5-004). [U9.2/26, U9.2/49: core repeat kept in full | U9.14/17 PART: recall B8 S01 "the names have degrees, a great and a greatest name" (B8 S02: every human a great cosmic name), then new: prophets and awliya His greatest name, Ali the greatest-greatest, in AH P018:1-5, 34-36 | RR5-004 PART: recall B1 S11 "Ali followed the Prophet like a young camel its mother", then new: the food story and Ali the full-length mirror of the Prophet, AZ-S23 ¶150-151]
  3. Does Ali say it of himself, and does the Quran name him? Yes: on 78:1-2 al-Baqir tells Abu Hamza that the Great News is Ali, who said «ما لله آية أكبر مني» (answer only, EZ P119:12-21, P120:19-22 with AH P025:4-7); he is the Prophet's own sign as the staff was Moses' («لكل نبي آية وهذا آية ربي», EZ P121:2-4; Ghadir or Iftitah tail, one of the two, EZ P121:5-7 or P121:12); 13:43 «ومن عنده علم الكتاب» if the narration is found (EZ P121:11) (U9.2/52, U9.2/15 first half, U9.2/53; Teacher Copy: U9.2/93 EZ P179, U9.2/94 EZ P180:6-10, U9.2/97 EZ P184, RR1-004, RR1-034, RR3-040, RR4-013, RR4-015, RR3-027). [U9.2/52: core repeat kept in full, new part 78:1-2 read as Ali | U9.2/15 PART: recall B1 S03 "«ما لله آية أكبر مني» (AH, inline, on the Mi'raj)" and B3 S04, new part in step 6 | U9.2/53 PART: recall B3 S04 "Ali among the greatest signs", then new: «لكل نبي آية وهذا آية ربي», EZ P121:2-4 | U9.2/94 PART: recall B1 S01 and B3 S02 "«إنما العلم ثلاثة», the firm sign read as nature pointing to God", then new: the firm sign read as Ali, EZ P180:6-10 (marked as the lecturer's reading) | U9.2/97 PART: recall B1 S12 "Ali rabbani of the umma", then new: the woman at the Kaaba, «علم الأعلام», EZ P184 | U9.2/93 NEW (TC)]
  4. What does "greatest" mean? The maker's finest work says most of him; and they also speak about Him, as sun and ant, which only show that He is, cannot («سلوني» once, in the H4 form); so the Infallibles are God's great work at whom all wonder, and none knew God truly but them (JS P055:20-34 under the recall; JS P066 to P068 with EZ P070 one line; JS P126:3-4 or EZ P115:11-12; EZ P138; U9.2/27, U9.2/28, U9.2/58, U9.2/66). [U9.2/27 SAME: recall only | U9.2/28 PART: recall B1 S11 "knowing the Imam grows tawhid" and B3 S04 "a greater sign than sun or cherubim", then new: the reason, they explain tawhid, JS P066 to P068 | U9.2/58 NEW | U9.2/66 PART: recall B1 S11 "none knew God truly but the Prophet and Ali (AH U1.3-1/P005, with EZ's form naming Fatima)", then new: the Infallibles as God's great work at whom all wonder, EZ P138]
  5. Which sign is greater than all? The proof of God: al-Askari to Ishaq b. Isma'il, after 20:125-126, «وأي آية أعظم من حجة الله على خلقه»: the blind of heart is the one who turns from it (EZ P125:8-13, key text of the half); al-Sadiq on 29:49 «نحن الآيات» (EZ P110:30-32); a wali becomes His greatest sign (JS P168); denying His signs comes from chosen sin, 30:10 (EZ P178) (U9.2/56, U9.2/57, U9.2/85; Teacher Copy: U9.2/92, U9.2/98, U9.2/59, U9.2/90, U9.2/95). [U9.2/56: core repeat kept in full, new part al-Askari's own words | U9.2/57 PART: recall B3 S04 (and B1 S10, EZ) "the Infallibles are God's greatest signs", then new: al-Sadiq's «نحن الآيات» on 29:49 (al-Baqir on 7:40 Teacher Copy only, Ask) | U9.2/85 PART: recall B3 S04, then new: the wali or first act as His greatest sign, JS P168 one sentence | U9.2/98 PART: recall B4 S04 "the House of 3:96 is inwardly the house of Muhammad and his family (EZ, TC)", then new: al-Sadiq to Abu Hanifa, only if Jami's wording is used | U9.2/92, U9.2/59, U9.2/90, U9.2/95 NEW (TC, each with a cut Ask)]
  6. Is Fatima too a greatest sign, and how do we weigh that? Ali says it of himself, and truly; God says it of Fatima, and that is a world of difference; 74:35 with three oaths and «إنّ», and «كبرى» as the feminine of «أكبر»; praise weighs as much as its giver, and here the giver is God; she is siddiqa kubra in her whole being, and at the resurrection «غضوا أبصاركم» because her luminosity is unveiled (AH P025:8-25, P160:93-108, P161:1-36, P026:4-10 with EZ P138:15-16 for the second clause, P027:1-23; U9.2/15 second half, U9.2/79, U9.2/80, U9.2/16, U9.2/17; Teacher Copy: U9.2/67, U9.2/81, U9.2/82, U9.2/19). [U9.2/15 PART new part: Fatima's greatness as God states it, AH P025:8-25 | U9.2/79 PART (verdict changed, see Ask 12): recall B3 S06 "AH: by the narrations the «ها» of 74:35 is Fatima; the oaths show the moon, the night's last third and the dawn are great signs", then new: three oaths plus «إنّ», so she is surely one of the kubra signs, AH P160:93-104 | U9.2/80 PART: recall B1 S11 "EZ: the higher the servant, the more his humility shows God's greatness", then new: AH's form, the greater the describer the clearer the described's greatness, AH P161:1-36 | U9.2/16, U9.2/17 NEW | U9.2/67, U9.2/81, U9.2/82, U9.2/19 NEW (TC)]

Who carries it

EZ carries steps 1, 3 and 5 and the Teacher Copy layer of the -b half; JS carries steps 2 and 4 (the Prophet first, Ali after him; the watch; sun and ant); AH carries step 6 (Fatima, P025 to P027, P160 to P163), the names ladder in step 2 (U9.14/17) and one line of Ali's own word (P025:4-7). MM is absent from the whole lesson (absent from U9.2). AH/MM-thin in steps 1, 3, 4 and 5: the AH re-read records placed here are mostly recalls (6 SAME) or off-subject Imamat lines; the new AH re-read teaching is RR5-004 (step 2), RR4-015 and the TC records RR1-004, RR1-034, RR3-040, RR4-013 (step 3). Nothing is borrowed to fill it.

Next lesson picks up

«کیا وہ خدا کی خاص صفات کے مظہر ہیں، اور ان کی پہچان خدا کو پہچنواتی ہے یا اس کی جگہ لے لیتی ہے؟» (Do they show particular attributes of His, and does knowing them make God known or replace Him?) (S05)

S05 «معصومین خدا کی خاص صفات کے مظہر ہیں، مگر وہ بندے ہیں جو اس کی یاد دلاتے ہیں، خدا نہیں؛ خدا خدا ہی سے پہچانا جاتا ہے»

ledger · 17 new · 9 part-taught before · 6 taught before · 1 kept in full
The steps 8
  1. Do they show particular attributes of God, beyond being His greatest sign (S04)? Yes: His creation is pure and glorifies Him; voluntary glorification is seen in the Prophet and Ali, whose justice, character, knowledge, forbearance and courage show the Pure God (EZ P078:4-8, P078:13, with 61:1); Husayn the mazhar of all perfections (JS P077:1-4); each Imam shows one quality more, all have all, as in the salawat of Khwaja Nasir, a scholar's composition («حسینی شجاعت», not «الحسین الشجاع», EZ P096:11-29, P097:12-15; JS P095:1-4). (U9.2/31, U9.2/37, U9.2/32 TC; RR3-021, RR4-023 TC). [U9.2/31 PART: recall B3 S04 "the Imams are His greatest sign" then new: they show Him by their perfections, Husayn the mazhar of all perfections, in EZ P078:6-8, JS P077:1-4 | U9.2/37 NEW | U9.2/32 PART: recall B3 S04 "the Imam a sign of God" then new: only the recited verse (EZ P082:5-7), fine layer, ASK]
  2. Which mercy is shown in Hasan and Husayn? They are the two portions of His mercy (57:28), each a mercy in his own way. (AH P023:1-6, P024:1-3, P024:53; EZ P106:1-3 «إنكما من ريحان الله» one line; EZ P107:6-11 «for the umma» fine layer). (U9.2/14, U9.2/45, U9.2/44, U9.2/20 TC; RR2-001, RR2-002, RR5-034, RR5-050, RR5-035, RR5-051, RR2-004). [U9.2/14 SAME: recall only "Recall B1 S11: the two portions of mercy in 57:28 are Hasan and Husayn" | U9.2/45 SAME: recall only | U9.2/44 NEW (one line) | U9.2/20 NEW, TC, ASK | RR5-035 PART: recall B1 S11 "Hasan's truce and Husayn's rising both mercy" then new: the nine Imams carry both bloods, the essence of the two mercies, the Imam of the Age their quintessence (AH MJ-S08 ¶165-168), TC]
  3. Why may the sinner hope? Husayn is the mazhar of God's vast mercy and kindness: al-Hurr's sin was beyond measure, yet he was received without shame; so none should despair, and God's own mercy is beyond even His mazhar's (JS P083:5-41, P084:4-15 told once with JS P166, U9.2-7; JS P086:3 «سفينة النجاة» one line; P086 tawassul frame held for U9.16). (U9.2/33, U9.2/84, U9.2/34). [U9.2/33 PART: recall B7 S23 "do not despair of His mercy; Husayn's rising a portion of mercy (TC)" then new: Husayn the mazhar of mercy as the ground of hope, in JS P083:27-28, P084:10-11 | U9.2/84 PART: recall B7 S02 "no creaturely kindness reaches His mercy" (also B7 S23, B8 S12) then new: His mercy beyond even His mazhar's, in JS P166 (the closing «پس خدا بہت مہربان ہے») | U9.2/34 NEW, one line]
  4. Does one Imam stand for one quality? Yes, by the conditions of his time: al-Rida the mazhar of compassion, «through him God pleased whom He willed» (JS P088:10-22, the Friday salawat); the Imam of the Age the mazhar of love, carrying the Prophet's «عزيز عليه ما عنتم» (EZ P089:18-27, 9:128). (U9.2/35; RR2-041). [U9.2/35 NEW]
  5. And Ali? The mazhar of His highest mercy and of His overpowering wrath, as God is «أرحم الراحمين في موضع العفو والرحمة وأشد المعاقبين» (JS P090:2-12); and the one who bows most before that wrath: the perfect man's special humility before the tajalli of qahr, Ali's swoon (AH P012:1-13); his birth inside the Ka'ba a sign, «وأنا موقنة أنه إحدى آياتك» (EZ P143:10-12, JS P142:14-15, two lines). (U9.2/36 first half, U9.2/10, U9.2/68; U9.2/69, U9.2/41, U9.2/89 TC). [U9.2/36 NEW | U9.2/10 PART: recall B1 S18 "AH tells Ali's fainting as true: from knowing comes fear" then new: read as the perfect man's humility before His subduing, the jalal side of the mirror, in AH P012:1-10 | U9.2/68 NEW]
  6. What else does the household show? The Imam the locus of divine 'isma, «فالحق ما رضيتموه» (EZ P092:2-3 only; obedience material is Imamat); the mazhar of God's ghayra (EZ P169, speaker to settle; P170 TC); Fatima, whose light the Light verse likens to God's (AH RR3-030, RR3-031 recalled; RR3-032 new: the glass, not the lamp, shines like a star), a system of knowledge (AH RR3-034, TC), her light reaching her descendants (AH RR1-026, ASK); the Mahdi the mazhar of His vast power, if Ali confirms P102 as EZ's (EZ P102); the Prophet named in the Lawh His light, envoy and veil (EZ P101:9 after the Jami check; JS P099 one line, see Texts), pointer to U9.12/U9.13. (U9.2/36 second half, U9.2/86, U9.2/87 TC, U9.2/88 TC, U9.2/40, U9.2/39; RR3-030, RR3-031, RR3-032, RR3-034, RR1-026, RR1-027, RR5-029). [U9.2/86 PART: recall B4 S17 "EZ, al-Sajjad's pulpit in Sham, for the true monotheists" then new: «we are the mazhar of God's ghayra», EZ P169 | U9.2/87 NEW, TC | U9.2/88 NEW, TC | U9.2/40 NEW if confirmed | U9.2/39 PART: recall B6 S06 "MM reads the glittering veil of the Mi'raj as the Prophet's body" then new: the Prophet named His veil and His light, EZ P101:9, JS P099:1, P099:45-46]
  7. Does such greatness cross into godhood? No: their servanthood is itself part of the sign (EZ P074:4-15, P071:16; JS P073:6-13); they are His names, and a name shows what it names, so they are names and signs at once (JS P075:4-13), but not God, and words that give them godhood are the other deviation (EZ P076:2-6, P076:13-16). (U9.2/29, U9.2/30; RR3-033, RR1-022, RR3-012, RR3-042). [U9.2/29 SAME: recall only "Recall B1 S11: the higher the servant, the more his bowing shows God's greatness" | U9.2/30 PART: recall B1 S11 "sign, name and khalifa in knowing God (EZ)", B3 S04 "the Imams His greatest signs", B8 S01 "a name is a sign of the named" then new: the Imams at once His names and His signs, in JS P075:4-13, EZ P076:2-5]
  8. Then does knowing them make God known, or take His place? Look first at what God made in Ali, a work that shows its Maker as no one but Muhammad does (EZ P130:10-31); yet God is known by God and Ali reminds (EZ P132:1-5; JS P131:2-6 «ما عرفت الله بمحمد بل عرفت محمدا بالله»); the general knowledge is by God, the detail of His perfections through the proofs (EZ P134:2-10; JS P133:3-12, al-Saduq's gloss in Urdu); the more power a servant is known to have, the greater God (EZ P135:10, P137:1-11); the Prophet kissed Fatima's hand attending to what God made (EZ P187:17-28); so hear these perfections and say «جل جلاله» (EZ P188:8-9), and know Ali more to serve God more, not Ali (EZ P136:14-15; «أنا عبد من عبيد محمد» P128:23 one line). The Fasl's landing and its guard against ghuluw (detail U9.11, held). (U9.2/60, U9.2/61, U9.2/62, U9.2/63, U9.2/64, U9.2/65 TC, U9.2/99, U9.2/100, U9.2/101 TC). [U9.2/60 NEW | U9.2/61 core repeat kept in full, ASK | U9.2/62 SAME: recall only | U9.2/63 SAME: recall only | U9.2/64 PART: recall B1 S11 "knowing the Imam increases tawhidic knowledge" then new: it leads to serving God, not Ali, EZ P136:14-15 | U9.2/65 SAME: recall only | U9.2/99 NEW | U9.2/100 NEW | U9.2/101 NEW, TC, ASK]

Texts and Jami results

(1) 57:28 «يُؤْتِكُمْ كِفْلَيْنِ مِن رَّحْمَتِهِ» (AH P023; reprinted, flagged B1 S08). (2) al-Hurr: Jami has «فترى لي من ذلك توبة؟ فقال له الحسين: نعم يتوب الله عليك فانزل» (al-Irshad p. 100; Bihar 45:11); JS's «هل لي من توبة» is another wording and «ارفع رأسك» is NOT-FOUND in Jami (texts table rows 27, 28): print the Jami form, JS's words as his telling. (3) The salawat «... صاحب الدعوة النبوية والصولة الحيدرية والعصمة الفاطمية ...» (Muhaj al-da'awat 1:430; Zad al-ma'ad 1:404): Khwaja Nasir's composition, not a masum text; the print «والصلاة» is a garble of «والصولة» (row 31). (4) The Friday salawat on al-Rida «الذي ارتضيته ورضيت به من شئت من خلقك» (Misbah al-mutahajjid p. 404; Bihar 91:76), found; 9:128 inline. (5) Du'a al-Iftitah «أرحم الراحمين في موضع العفو والرحمة» (found; reprinted, flagged B1 S03 and B7 S22). (6) Fatima bint Asad «وأنا موقنة أنه إحدى آياتك ودلائلك» (Bihar 35:36-37; Hilyat al-abrar 2:21), found in another wording. (7) «ما عرفت الله بمحمد بل عرفت محمدا بالله» (al-Tawhid; found) with «لولا الله ما عرفنا ولولا نحن ما عرف الله» (al-Tawhid, cited by al-Saduq; found); «فبه عرفناه» and «ولولا الله ما عرف الحجج» are al-Saduq's own gloss (rows 38, 39): in Urdu, as his explanation. Also: the Lawh of Jabir reads «لمحمد نبيه ونوره وسفيره وحجابه ودليله» (al-Ghayba 1:47; al-Ihtijaj p. 164), the lesson's «ووسیه» is a garble of «وسفيره», and the restore P101/10 is al-Mazandarani's commentary, not the Lawh (row 32); JS P099's «آيتك العظمى ... حجابك الأدنى» is a du'a Majlisi composed (Bihar 92:358-359), not Ali's (row 33).

Next lesson picks up

«سب سے پہلے کیا پیدا ہوا، اور خدا کی مشیت کا ظرف کون ہے؟» (What was created first, and who is the vessel of God's will?) (S06) (RR 21: SAME recalled 14, PART 2, NONE 5.)

3. The first issued, creative wilaya, tafwid and its limit S06 to S09

S06 سب سے پہلے رسولِ اکرم صلی اللہ علیہ و آلہ کا نور صادر ہوا اور وہی اللہ کی مشیت کا ظرف ہے، اور ائمہ علیہم السلام کے دل اس کے ارادے کا مورد ہیں؛ اللہ کا جلال ہر ایک سے براہِ راست رابطے سے انکار کرتا ہے، اس لیے یہی نور سب سے بڑا حجاب ہے، اور اسی کے واسطے سے اس کی ہر عطا درجہ بدرجہ ایسی حقیقی ملکیت بن کر پہنچتی ہے جو اللہ کی ملکیت کو کم نہیں کرتی

ledger H06 · 9 new · 28 part-taught before · 2 taught before · 0 kept in full
The steps 6
  1. What did God create first? The light of the Prophet: the first issued, the first intellect and the first knower of God, the head of the whole order, «الخاتم لما سبق والفاتح لما استقبل», the one who completes and the one who opens; all the worlds and their beings are from his light; it is "Your mercy that encompasses all things" (Du'a Kumayl); at the apex of the cone of being there is no creature between God and creation but his station of light; and the Fourteen are pillars of creation, of whom one does not speak to one whose belief is not yet sound (MM P001:2 as MM's own preface salutation, not a Masum text; MM P002:2; MM P003:1-2 with «لولاك لما خلقت الأفلاك» one line, late, Ask; MM P003:3 ziyara block; MM P006:2, P008:2-3; AH P048:9-10 and P199:1-2 the Jabir report, once, in Jami's wording; AH P198:1, P199:1-3, P200:1-2; EZ P131:1-4; JS U9.15-2 P056:1-17 with P058:3-8 as the proposed cut; points /1, /2, /7, /82, /111, U9.15/22; /112 and RR5-015, RR5-003, RR1-014 Teacher Copy only). ↺ B1 S11 · B2 S17Already taught: B1 S11 "al-Baqir: «فنحن أول خلق الله ... ونحن سبب خلق الخلق», we are God's first creation and the cause of creation (MM), with 'for your sake I began creation' and EZ's first-light lines; B2 S17 the Prophet first to answer"New here: the Prophet as al-sadir al-awwal and the perfecting last (MM P003:2-3), the first intellect and first knower (MM P002:2), the whole world from his light as the ground of creative wilaya (EZ P131), his light read as the all-encompassing mercy (AH P198 to P200, base B7 S23 7:156 as His general mercy), JS's "pillars of creation" and the caution (P056:5-13)
  2. What is God's will, and is it His essence? His knowledge is in the rank of the Essence, where there is no determining; the determining is the rank of act, by His ra'y: as bare existence it is mashiyya, as its traits irada, as its measures taqdir; and these are the very determination of the first issued, the light of the Seal; Imam Rida's lamp: the light of the lamp is not an act of it, so the first issued by His ra'y is the Prophet's light; the Kafi report «علم وشاء وأراد ... والعلم متقدم على المشيئة»; AH: the will is the light and existence of the first emanated, an act of God in the outside world, never His Essence; «خلق الله المشيئة بنفسها ثم خلق الأشياء بالمشيئة» (Kafi order), so whatever is after the will took existence through his station of light (MM P003:1, P003:4-6, P004:1-2; MM P007:2 lamp block shortened to its last lines and P007:3; MM P009:9-10 kept only to «والعلم متقدم على المشيئة»; AH P026:1-4; EZ P129:1-15, P130:3-12; points /3, /7, /25, /81; /5 and /9 Teacher Copy only). ↺ B7 S14 · B7 S15Already taught: B7 S14 "mashiyya is His act, an attribute of act, the filter every gift passes through (JS, EZ); B7 S15 maps mashiyya and irada"New here: will, intention and measure as the determination of the first emanated (MM P003:1-2, P004:1), AH's identification of the will with the first emanated's light and existence (P026:1-2), the will as the station of the Prophet's light (EZ P129:9-15). The lamp hadith and MM P120:3 already stand in B7 S17 (see Repeats, /7)
  3. Then who is the vessel of His will? The being of the Seal: in luminosity his rank is the rank of the will, not of the beings; in the world of shadows he is the vessel of the will and the clear book; and God made the Imams' hearts the place of His will: «إن الله جعل قلوب الأئمة موردا لإرادته فإذا شاء الله شيئا شاؤوه» (76:30); so the Imams are His power and will («إن الأئمة من آل محمد قدرة الله ومشيته», «يا موضع مشية الله», late, Ask); AH's glass: a vessel is worth what it holds, and what fills them is God's will («قلوبنا أوعية لمشية الله فإذا شاء شئنا» in Jami's wording, AH's print «نحن أوعية مشية الله»); the malakut of all things is in God's hand (23:88) and He gives of it to His friends, always by His leave (MM P004:1 «نحن مشية الله», late, Ask; MM P006:2; MM P008:3; MM P010:2 block (key); MM P011:4-5; AH P028:1-2 (23:88; 38:47 under Ask), P028:8, P028:16-28; points /4, /6, /8, /10, /11, /27). [PART for /4, /6, /8, /27: recall B7 S14 (mashiyya His act), B2 S15 and B2 S18 (the world of shadows), B4 S07 (God alone owns and grants the Prophet and Imams what they hold) then new: the Seal's being as the will, his rank in luminosity as the rank of the will, the vessel of the will, 23:88 given to His friends. NEW for /10, /11: taught in full]
  4. Why is there a veil between God and creation at all? His majesty, greatness, grandeur and rule refuse direct contact with every unfit one, so He sets intermediaries, many of them; the Seal is the greatest veil, Amir al-Mu'minin the second, then the Imams; a veil of light is like the glare of a lamp that keeps the eye from what is behind it; each issued being is a veil for the one after it, seventy thousand veils and more, and only the first issued has no veil between him and God but his own holy self (AH P015:1-11; AH P018:11-19, P018:29-33 lamp only, the cone of rays P018:25-28 under Ask; AH P012:26-36; AH P013:11-24; points /14, /13 (P013:13-24 part)). ↺ B1 S09 · B8 S11Already taught: B1 S09 "God does not teach people directly by His Essence, so a prophet is needed as intermediary (AH); B8 S11 the king who does not deal with the street sweeper"New here: majesty as the reason, and the intermediaries as the greatest veil, ontological, not only for teaching (P015:1-7, P012:26-36)
  5. How does what God gives reach the rest? Rank by rank: the Seal takes from the light of His greatness, Ali owns from the Seal's light by God's leave, the Imams from both, then prophets, saints, angels and believers, each from the one above by his consent and in the measure he wills; so there is one wilaya, the divine, coming down, and each higher being is the qayyum of the lower by God's qayyumiyya; this is the order of tamlik in the arc of descent, the Prophet a cause by God's grace (AH P016:2-17 with «أنا أديب الله وعلي أديبي» once, as «منسوب», under the recall; AH P019:1-12; AH P051:1-9, P052:5-12 with 5:55 once; AH U9.16-1 P013:1-12 with the garbled P013:4 under Ask; points /15, /18, /45, U9.16/12). [PART for /18, /45; SAME for /15: recall only; NEW for U9.16/12]
  6. Is that ownership real, and does it make the creature share in God's? Real: we find in ourselves real power, knowledge and life once we own the light of wilaya, and on this real ownership rest otherness, createdness and the veil; the first issued, owning God's light, knowledge and power, is a being truly other than God; but when sight turns to His greater ownership there is no room to see the creature's ownership, though it is not cancelled; and His ownership stays to the last creature; so even the first issued is subbuh and quddus, and how much more God: «سبح اسم ربك الأعلى» (87:1) (AH P012:1-25; AH P013:1-10; AH P019:1-2 last clause; AH P021:1-10; points /12, /13, /20; /21 Teacher Copy only, AH P022). [PART for /12, /13: recall B1 S13 "ownership is real (AH)", B4 S07 "God alone owns essentially, creatures hold on loan by His grant (AH)", B3 S08 "even the Prophet's light is needy and not God (JS)" then new: seeing His greater ownership leaves no room to see the creature's, which is not cancelled (P012:1-12); the first emanated owning His light, knowledge and power while a creature truly other than God (P013:9-10). NEW for /20: taught in full] Close (one line, step 6): the next lesson asks what this power looks like in the hands of the wali, what creative wilaya is and who holds it.

Texts and Jami results 7
  1. «إِنَّ اللهَ جَعَلَ قُلُوبَ الْأَئِمَّةِ مَوْرِداً لِإِرَادَتِهِ فَإِذَا شَاءَ اللهُ شَيْئاً شَاؤُوهُ» with 76:30 (Abu al-Hasan; Tafsir al-Qummi 2:409, Bihar 5:114, Basa'ir via Mustadrak Safina 4:251; Jami FOUND). The key text of step 3.
  2. «لَا يَكُونُ السُّكُوتُ إِلَّا عَنْ نُطْقٍ قَبْلَهُ ...» (Imam Rida to Imran al-Sabi; al-Tawhid, Bihar 10:313-314; FOUND; printed in full in B7 S17) with the Kafi «علم وشاء وأراد ... والعلم متقدم على المشيئة» (al-'Alim, Imam Kazim; al-Kafi, bab al-bada'; FOUND) and «خلق الله المشيئة بنفسها ثم خلق الأشياء بالمشيئة» (Imam Sadiq; al-Kafi 1:162; FOUND-OTHER-WORDING: EZ prints the reverse order).
  3. «اَلْخَاتَمِ لِمَا سَبَقَ وَاَلْفَاتِحِ لِمَا اِسْتَقْبَلَ» (ziyara of the Prophet; Misbah al-Za'ir 1:121, Farhat al-Ghari 1:107-108; FOUND).
  4. The Jabir report: Jami has «أول شيء خلق الله ما هو؟ فقال: نور نبيك يا جابر، خلقه الله ثم خلق منه كل خير» (Bihar 15:24, 54:170; Mustadrak Safina 2:14; late sources) and a separate late form «يا جابر أول ما خلق الله نور نبيك» (Ghurar al-akhbar); AH's print joins them (FOUND-OTHER-WORDING, Ask 5). With «اللهم إني أسألك برحمتك التي وسعت كل شيء» (Du'a Kumayl; Iqbal 3:331; FOUND; printed in full in B7 S23).
  5. «قلوبنا أوعية لمشية الله فإذا شاء شئنا» (the Imam of the Age; Kamal al-din via Mustadrak Safina 4:252; FOUND-OTHER-WORDING for AH's «نحن أوعية مشية الله») with «قُلْ مَنْ بِيَدِهِ مَلَكُوتُ كُلِّ شَيْءٍ» (23:88).
  6. «إِنَّ الأَئِمَّةَ مِنْ آلِ مُحَمَّدٍ قُدْرَةُ اللهِ وَمَشِيَّتُهُ» and «يَا مَوْضِعَ مَشِيَّةِ اللهِ» (only Mustadrak Safina 6:100, a one-line summary and "in some ziyarat", late) and «نحن مشيّة الله» (only Mustadrak Safina 6:99 from Majma' al-nurayn, late). Step 3 therefore rests on text 1 and text 5 (diag Q20).
  7. «سَبِّحِ اسْمَ رَبِّكَ الْأَعْلَى» (87:1, once) and «إِنَّمَا وَلِيُّكُمُ اللهُ وَرَسُولُهُ ...» (5:55, once, step 5); «أنا أديب الله وعلي أديبي» (Makarim al-akhlaq 1:17; FOUND) as «منسوب», under the /15 recall.

جب اللہ کی مشیت ان ہستیوں کے وجود کے ظرف میں آ جاتی ہے تو ولیِ خدا کے ہاتھ پر یہ قدرت کیسے ظاہر ہوتی ہے: ولایتِ تکوینی کیا ہے، کیسے ملتی ہے اور کس کے پاس ہے؟ (S07)

S07 «ولایت تکوینی خدا کی ولایت کا وہ نمونہ ہے جو بندگی کے راستے سے ملتا ہے: جانوں اور چیزوں پر ان سے زیادہ حق رکھنا، خدا کے اذن سے؛ یہ چودہ معصومین علیہم السلام میں ہے اور آج امام زمانہ عجل اللہ تعالیٰ فرجہ الشریف کے وجود میں، اور معجزے اس کا چھوٹا سا جلوہ ہیں، ان کی اصل شان نہیں»

The steps 9
  1. How is one sent from God known? By signs of divine knowledge and divine power, both given and not earned; so a claimant to succession too must do in creation what an educated man cannot, and the Imams keep to nature's course and break it only to prove their truth. (AH P062:1-10, P063:1-23, P064:1-24 with 57:25 and 2:253 first clause; EZ H8 P187:8-12, H7 P142:5-13; U9.14/53, /89, /104; Teacher Copy U9.14/54 (AH P065, water from his fingers, one figure), /91). [U9.14/53 PART: recall B1 S10 "God introduces His Prophet by signs (the lizard and the pebble)" then new: AH's rule that one sent from God must bear divine knowledge and power, P062 to P064 | U9.14/89 PART: recall B3 S12 "AH's bayyina tells true prophets, and to a degree true successors, from false claimants" then new: EZ's test that a claimant of succession must act in creation, H7 P142:5-13 | U9.14/104 PART: recall B3 S12 "breaking nature's custom is the bayyina of a true prophet or successor (AH)" then new: the Imams follow nature and break it only to prove the truth; once proved it is not repeated, EZ H8 P187:8-12 | U9.14/54 NEW, Teacher Copy | U9.14/91 PART, Teacher Copy: recall B3 S12 then new: the Sunnis' denial (polemic, Ask 21)] AH's own re-read on this step is all recall (RR3-005, RR3-006, RR3-007, RR3-026, RR2-032, RR2-038; RR4-032 and RR3-041 Teacher Copy).
  2. Can things be changed by attention alone, without natural means? It is neither rationally nor customarily impossible; what changes is accident, not substance; matter is wax in His hand and in the hand of whichever servant He wills; the world does not resist God's friends, and the power is theirs by grant. (AH P029:4-7; AH P053:4-17 with 6:18 (complete the translation) as the recalled base; EZ H8 P151:11-15, P152:18-29; RR2-059; Teacher Copy RR2-030, RR2-031; U9.14/29, /46, /92). [U9.14/29 PART (changed from NONE, doubt read): recall B3 S09 and B3 S12 "the soul acts on matter by attention; the matter of the world is under the command of God's proofs, who do by attention outside what we do in imagination (AH)" then new: the rule itself as the major premise of the prophets' signs, not impossible rationally or customarily, change in accidents not substance, P029:4-7 | U9.14/46 SAME (changed from NONE, doubt read): recall only, B3 S15 | U9.14/92 PART: recall B3 S12 then new: EZ's wax image for the whole world, galaxies to angels and jinn, and the beasts that bowed, H8 P151:11-15, P152:18-29 | RR2-059 PART: new: turning the earth over is for the wali like a glance at a wall, his own body kept from ageing, healing others by attention]
  3. How does a servant reach it, and how is it unlike the soul-power of ascetics? By servanthood: who becomes a servant is filled with divine attributes, and wilaya, dominance and overturning things is one of them; first servanthood, then wilaya, then mission; the Prophet's servanthood was perfect, so he is the perfect wali; and God first prepares the one He chooses. Power from riyada is acquired and open to anyone; the gift without riyada is the bayyina. (AH P030:1-2, P031:2-3, P039:4-7, P040:4-9; EZ H8 P172; Teacher Copy JS P095, P108 for U9.14/73; U9.14/30, /38, /39, /101). [U9.14/30 NEW | U9.14/38 NEW | U9.14/39 NEW | U9.14/101 PART: recall B7 S14 "God chooses His friends, knowing their outcome (B7 S10)" then new: He first prepares the one He chooses, EZ H8 P172 | U9.14/73 NEW, Teacher Copy] The whole soul-power ladder from the re-read (RR2-035, RR2-036, RR2-037, RR2-060, RR2-067, RR5-056, RR5-057, RR5-058, RR5-059, RR5-067, RR5-068) is recall only: B1 S17 and B3 S12 teach it.
  4. What is wilaya? God's universal wilaya changes heaven, earth and man, and He places a sample of it in the prophets and successors; the Prophet's question at Ghadir, «أَلَسْتُ أَوْلَى بِكُمْ مِنْ أَنْفُسِكُمْ», means this creative wilaya: he is nearer to us, more knowing of us, more able over us than we are, and his will rules ours; and the same holds for Ali, over all contingent things. EZ: God is more entitled to dispose of you than you are (39:42, first clause), and the Imam holds the same; wilaya from wala, adjacency, in one analogy. (AH P034, P035, P038:1-4 (5:55 once), P043:8 the Ghadir line only, P044:2-6, P046:1-7; EZ H8 P158:8-18; EZ H11 P245:26-38 or H7 P140:1-8, one only; Teacher Copy RR2-017, RR4-020; U9.14/34, /35, /42, /96, /143). [U9.14/34 PART: recall B1 S06 "by the wilaya that is His, God turns our inner and outer states moment by moment (AH); B7 S11 His unlimited dominion over heaven and earth" then new: His universal wilaya as the source of its sample in His friends, P034 | U9.14/35 NEW | U9.14/42 PART (changed from NONE): recall B2 S08 "the cause is nearer to the effect than the effect to itself; the Prophet, in the rank of cause, is more entitled to us than ourselves (AH, Teacher Copy)" then new: Ghadir's question read as creative wilaya over all contingent things and Ali holding the same awla, P044:2-6, P046:1-7 | U9.14/96 NEW | U9.14/143 NEW] AH's own Ghadir re-read (RR2-014, RR2-015, RR2-018) is recall only (B2 S08).
  5. Who holds it, and where is it now? Takwini wilaya, a power by God's leave to overturn things, is in the Prophet, Fatima and the twelve Imams; before the Seal it was partial in the prophets, in him it is universal, and all the prophets are his community (56:10 once); all the Fourteen share his absolute creative wilaya ("you are to me as Harun to Musa"); Ali holds it from the Throne to beneath the carpet; the Prophet opened it through Ali, and it is now in the Imam of the Age, whose dua calls him God's absolute wali. (AH P031:1-4 with «مقلب القلوب والابصار» inline, P032, P038:3-4, P040:10-21, P041 (gaps, Ask 14), P042:1-2 (full salutation); AH H9 P203:15-22, H10 P224:1-9; EZ H7 P138:5-12, H12 P246:10; RR1-010, RR2-027, RR4-016, RR1-015, RR4-042; Teacher Copy RR1-011, RR2-016; U9.14/31, /32, /37, /40, /41, /55, /88, /114, /128, /144; Teacher Copy U9.14/43 recall, /44, /97, /98, /99, /108, /110, /113, /115). [U9.14/31 PART: recall B3 S12 "God's friends do nothing without His leave and everything with it (AH, the two loaves)" and B8 S16 (weak, plan only) then new: takwini wilaya named, defined, and placed in the Prophet, Fatima and the twelve Imams, P031:1-4 | U9.14/32 NEW | U9.14/37 NEW | U9.14/40 NEW (the Yad Allah reading held for U9.8) | U9.14/41 NEW | U9.14/55 NEW (its dry-tree carrier unsourced, Ask 12) | U9.14/88 PART: recall B3 S04 "no sign bears the power of creation put to work in the Infallible" then new: all the Fourteen share the Prophet's absolute creative wilaya, EZ H7 P138 | U9.14/114 NEW | U9.14/128 NEW | U9.14/144 PART: recall B3 S04 then new: the Harun hadith as ground for Ali's same creative power, EZ H12 P246:10 | U9.14/43 SAME: recall only | U9.14/44, /97 PART, Teacher Copy | U9.14/98, /99, /108, /110 PART, /113, /115 Teacher Copy]
  6. What does it look like? Real acts, not tales and not physics: the lion of the curtain at Imam al-Rida's word, Ali's armour rings opened like wax ("by us God softened iron for Dawud"), the pebbles turned to gold by God's leave; the earth stilled ("I am the insan of al-Zalzala", 99:1-4), the Euphrates struck with the Prophet's staff, Qanbar's malakuti eye opened; Bilqis's throne and «كن فيكون». Three or four in the body, the rest in the Teacher Copy (Ask 9). (AH P055, P060, P061; AH H10 P209 to P211, P215, P221 to P222; EZ H8 P152 to P154; U9.14/47, /51, /52, /93, /118, /122, /126; the palms /123 recall; Teacher Copy U9.2/96, U9.14/59, /60, /61, /94, /95, /119, /120, /121, /124, /125, /129, /133, /134, /146, /147, /150, U9.15/63, /69 and the re-read stories). [U9.14/47 PART (changed from NONE): recall B3 S12 "the lion existed only by al-Rida's attention and vanished when it turned away (AH)" then new: the story itself, the bread made to fly, the curtain left whole, the lion as matter given form by his attention, P055 | U9.14/51 NEW | U9.14/52 NEW | U9.14/93 PART: recall B3 S12 "the friends show God's power; the miracles are not superstition (EZ)" then new: the world does not resist them; their miracles are not physical yet real, EZ H8 P152 to P154 | U9.14/118 PART: recall B8 S16 (plan only; the same report there for what God's names do, EZ) then new: AH's use of it for Ali's wilaya, the earth understands him | U9.14/122 PART: recall B1 S07 "creatures understand more than we do (AH)" then new: the Euphrates story, "God allowed me these two" | U9.14/126 NEW | U9.14/123 SAME: recall only]
  7. Does it reach those joined to them, and is it theirs or God's? It reaches by capacity: Rushayd's gulfs from Ali's ocean, Salman, Mary's wilaya over the palm (19:25 once); the ranks are prophets by degree, the friends limited, the Fourteen absolute and equal, "I take the last of you as I took the first"; the Rajab line "whatever You have You gave to them"; the wali turns time, hence Sahib al-Zaman. The power is granted, so theirs, but used only by leave: the Euphrates, two strikes allowed. (AH H10 P223:4-13, P223:81-84, P215:21, P216:14-16; EZ H7 P144:9-18, P146:5-14, P147:19-20, H11 P243:10, H12 P255 the Rajab line only; EZ U9.15-6 P150:18-27; U9.14/90, /127, /142, /145, /151, U9.15/62). [U9.14/90 NEW | U9.14/127 NEW | U9.14/142 NEW | U9.14/145 NEW | U9.14/151 PART: recall B3 S12 "from the same Rajab prayer, the friends show what His power is (EZ)" then new: the line "whatever You have You gave to them" for their creative wilaya | U9.15/62 NEW]
  8. Are the wonders their real station? No: denying miracles and the friends' wilaya shows weak faith, yet moving a mountain is nothing for the Prophet, and these are chickpeas and raisins thrown to children; their station is divine knowledge, taqwa and servanthood, and the highest power of Ali's wilaya was to hold dominion over mulk and malakut and bear every hardship in obedience. Foretelling events a thousand years ahead is itself their miracle. (AH P057, P058:1-4, P059:12-19 (without the unsourced dog story, Ask 5), P036:1-14, P074:1-4; RR1-036; U9.14/36, /49, /50; Teacher Copy U9.14/58). [U9.14/36 NEW | U9.14/49 PART: recall B3 S12 "EZ faults those who call the Imams' miracles superstition" then new: AH's verdict that denying miracles and the saints' wilaya is weak faith, P057 | U9.14/50 PART: recall B8 S16 (plan only, weak: the verses that move mountains are known to the Imams) then new: moving a mountain is nothing for the Prophet, P058:1-4 | U9.14/58 PART, Teacher Copy: recall B1 S12 "AH urges students to read Bihar" then new: volume 7 on the Imams | RR1-036 PART: new: such foretellings are the Imams' miracles] This step hands the restraint thread to S08 and S09 (AH P036 is pointed back to there).
  9. Close: creative wilaya is the sample of God's own wilaya, reached by servanthood and held by His leave; whether that means He has handed creation over to them is the next lesson.
