Next lesson picks up

if they hold this power, has God handed the running of creation over to them, and by whose leave do they act? «اگر ان کے پاس یہ قدرت ہے تو کیا خدا نے مخلوق کا کام انہیں سونپ دیا ہے، اور وہ کس کے اذن سے کرتے ہیں؟» (S08)

S08 اللہ نے اپنے اولیا کو اپنی مخلوق پر قدرت اور ملکیت دی ہے، مگر خود کنارہ نہیں کیا: یہ تفویض حق ہے، مطلق نہیں؛ وہ اسی کے اذن سے کرتے ہیں اور وہی چاہتے ہیں جو اللہ چاہے

ledger H08 · 14 new · 10 part-taught before · 1 taught before · 0 kept in full
The steps 7
  1. Has God handed creation over to them? Not in the sense that He gave it away and stepped aside: tafwid in creation of that kind is unbelief, the Imams rejected it and the tawqi's curse the mufawwida. Yet some things are truly entrusted: some laws (fard al-nabi: the five added rak'as, a kindness, not a burden) and some creative affairs. The Jawad hadith gives the true sense: «وأجرى طاعتهم عليها وفوّض أمورها إليهم ... ولن يشاؤوا إلا أن يشاء الله». Entrusting is by His leave, not handing over, and it is never absolute: even on the Night of Qadr, when the year's decrees are brought to the Imam of the Age for his signature, God says "carry it out when Our wali also wills it; We entrusted it, not absolutely". (EZ P079:7-11, P079:23-26, P079:33-46 (the minister example P079:12-15 and the travel rule P079:28-32 Teacher Copy); JS P077:1-3 with the verified block, P077:4-6, P080:2-11; EZ P081:15, P081:62-63; EZ H9 P192:40-48, the Qadr setting in one line from P192:30-38; P081:1-61 Teacher Copy or out, Ask 4; Teacher Copy: kept import U10.14.1, MM.) [U9.14/63 NEW | U9.14/64 NEW | U9.14/106 NEW]
  2. What then does "God made us able over whatever we will" mean? Al-Baqir binds their power to their will: «إن الله أقدرنا على ما نريد»; the earth's reins are in their hand; at his strike gold came out of the floor, and still Jabir was told "nothing for you now, you are being tried". Everything is humbled before them («وذلّ كل شيء لكم»). The Sahifa's first du'a says God gave them «الفضيلة بالملكة على جميع خلقه», every creature led to their obedience «بقدرته ... بعزته», God named inside the sentence. As vessels of His mashiyya they can split the moon. One limit: the power does not attach to the essentially impossible, which is no thing (recalled). (JS P082:3-9; P087:2-3 block trimmed to «سترنا عنكم أكثر مما أظهرنا لكم» and «إن الله أقدرنا على ما نريد», the gold scene in one line from P085:78-89 (Kumayt P085:34-72 out, Ask 7); P085:101-107; P087:7; EZ P088:2 one clause; JS P093:9-11, P094:1-3, P093:28-31, P094:8-25, P093:21-23; H11 JS P228:7-10, P229:4, P229:11 for the one telling of the Sahifa line; EZ H7 P133:14-22; JS H10 P226:7-19 recalled; Teacher Copy: JS P085:108-121 (balloons, 50:44), JS P091:16-20 with 27:40 first clause, Jaza'iri H11 P229:6-20, the university head H11 P230:1-20; kept import U10.14.14, AH.) [U9.14/65 PART: recall B8 S16 "the Imams, holding 72 letters of the Greatest Name, can do more than Asaf" then new: their own saying, as wilaya, in JS P082:3-9, P087:3, P085:101-107 | U9.14/66 NEW | U9.14/68 NEW | U9.14/84 PART: recall B7 S11 "the moon split named as a wonder within God's power" then new: as vessels of His mashiyya they can split it, in EZ H7 P133:14-22 | U9.14/67 PART (Teacher Copy): recall B8 S16 "Asaf brings Bilqis's throne, 27:40" then new: traversing the earth as a mark of their wilaya, JS P091:3-20 | U9.14/130 SAME: recall B7 S11 only | U9.14/131 NEW (Teacher Copy) | U9.14/132 NEW (Teacher Copy)]
  3. Is that shirk? No. God gives no one His own; whatever He gives is a sample, lent, and the reins of the Seal himself stay in His hand (the prayer-mat example, the teacher left unnamed). He grants ownership of power and keeps His own: He is «املک», can add more or take it all back; the owned never becomes the owner, but by the true Owner's leave the kingdom of the heavens and earth can be in its hands: that is the border of tawhid and shirk. Ali's own munajat says it: «أنت المالك وأنا المملوك». True tafwid is the father who gives his son the shop key, or the keys of the whole building, without registering the deed; false tafwid is the deed signed over and the father gone. (AH P028:9-15, P028:13-14 the core; EZ P099:2-6, P099:18-30, P099:22-24 with the munajat (Jami: Kufa mosque munajat, other wording); P099:31-36 Teacher Copy; EZ H7 P133:2-12; EZ P125:2-13, P125:20-26; EZ H7 P137:1-28, P137:33-35 merged with P125 as one telling; P137:29-32 and P137:36 Teacher Copy (Ask 9); Teacher Copy: RR1-042, RR3-025, RR4-029 under their recalls; kept import U10.14.2, MM.) [U9.14/28 PART: recall B1 S05 "God placed samples of His attributes in man" (and B4 S07 "creatures hold by grant") then new: God gives no one His own, whatever He gives is a sample, in AH P028:9-15 | U9.14/85 NEW | U9.14/87 NEW | U9.14/78 NEW (the key and the shop) | RR1-042, RR3-025, RR4-029 SAME: recall only]
  4. "We are our Lord's handiwork, and creation after is our handiwork": what does it mean? Made by them, made for them, or nurtured by them. JS joins the two wordings («صنائع ربنا ... صنائعنا» and «صنائع لنا») by "nurtured": God nurtured them directly, and people take every growth in tawhid, knowledge, character and law through them; EZ adds that it holds only as an ownership granted by God, all by His leave. (EZ P101:5-7, P104:6-13; JS P097:1-4, P098:5-8, P103:1-21; EZ P099:3 as the boundary; Ask 10 on the reading and Ask 11 on the speaker.) [U9.14/69 PART: recall B1 S11 "the Imams are the first creation; 'for your sake I began the creation'" then new: the sana'i' saying and the two readings, in EZ P101:5-7, P104:6-13, JS P103:3-21 | U9.14/70 NEW]
  5. By whose leave do they act? Always His. They ask God whether they have leave, and act by it; Jesus revived and healed "by God's leave", the breath was his, the effect was God's. The word «اذن» is in the narration itself: «قد أذن الله في فرجك» (the two loaves, recalled). The prophets' miracles belong to this same wilaya, within His leave: the Rajab du'a asks God «بما نطق فيهم من مشيتك». (EZ P116:3-9, P116:13-16; JS P115:19-27, P115:47-51; the loaves story JS P114:6-P115:18 recalled in one line; EZ H8 P171:2-8 with the Rajab block trimmed to «بما نطق فيهم من مشيتك»; «لا فرق بينك وبينها» held for U9.11; Teacher Copy: RR5-065, RR2-040, RR1-038 and RR2-068 under their recalls.) [U9.14/76 PART: recall B3 S12 "AH's story of Imam Zayn al-Abidin: the friends of God do nothing until God permits, everything once He permits" then new: EZ's and JS's statement of the principle, in EZ P116:3-9, JS P115:19-27 | U9.14/100 PART: recall B3 S12 "the practical bayyina opens the door of wilaya, a God-given power" then new: the prophets' miracles are part of this wilaya within His leave, in EZ H8 P171:2-8 | RR5-065, RR2-040, RR1-038, RR2-068 SAME: recall only]
  6. Then why not use it everywhere, with one glance on every opponent? One wilayi glance can transform a person, and the power is real; but the whole of existence is put only in the hands of one who encompasses it all, and power rests on knowledge: who knows what a deed will bring is the one who can do it. They too walk by the same general laws; of a hundred who asked perhaps one was granted, when God had permitted; had they met every need, faith would have become a free clinic. Their holding back is obedience, which S09 takes up. (EZ P111:1-5, P111:9-12; JS P109:1-13 (the tawassul lines P109:1-13 to U9.16, one clause kept); EZ P113:1-7; JS P112:1-15; RR5-064 ZH-S19 ¶103-108, slur ¶106 left out; EZ P117:9-25 and JS P115:34-43 point forward to S09; AH P036 already in S07, pointed back to.) [U9.14/74 NEW | U9.14/75 PART: recall B8 S16 "al-Sadiq's verses tie the Imams' power to their knowledge; Asaf's act by one letter of the Name" then new: power resting on knowledge as a general rule of their wilaya, in JS P112:7-15, EZ P113:4-5 | RR5-064 PART: base recalled (B3 S12, B4 S17), new: of a hundred perhaps one was granted]
  7. So their will is God's will, and obedience to them is obedience to Him. «إن الإمام وكر لإرادة الله عز وجل لا يشاء إلا ما شاء الله»; 76:30 recalled from S06; the Prophet's rule is God's rule (33:36). God made obedience to them binding on all things and joined it to His: the ziyara's «واسترعاكم أمر خلقه وقرن طاعتكم بطاعته», with 4:59 first clause. Even meeting the Imam of the Age is only as much as he wills, and he wills only what God permits (Teacher Copy). (JS P118:5-14, P119:1-2; EZ P120:3-19, P122:1-14, P123:1-7 (Zaynab at the tents, Teacher Copy); EZ P125:14-19 (its «إذا أراد أراد الله» NOT-FOUND, Ask 14); JS P105:6-8; JS P106:37-42; AH P033:12-19 Teacher Copy; Teacher Copy: kept import U10.14.3, MM.)   • Dropped U10.14.16 (AH: refuge in the Imam of the Age at the start of every sermon): no sound framing: refuge, U9.15 C and U9.16.
  • Dropped U10.14.17 (AH: in every age a pole, the channel of grace): no sound framing: U9.15.
  • Dropped U10.14.18 (JS: at the Imam's gesture a tree came forward and testified): no sound framing for this lesson: a wonder, S07's ground; JS only.
  • Dropped U10.14.19 (EZ: the reappearance is in God's hand only, not even the Imam's): no sound new support: EZ only, and the same point is the Halawi dream (RR1-038, RR2-068), already recalled from B7 S28.
  • Dropped U10.14.20 (EZ, JS list: the Sahifa, the Jami'a "everything humbled before you", the painted lion, the Greatest Name, Asif's throne, tay al-ard and more): no sound new support: its texts are this lesson's own (Sahifa, Jami'a: step 2) or S07's wonders; EZ/JS only.
  • Dropped U10.14.21 (EZ: the return of the sun and its defence; the Prophet could split the moon and return the sun): no sound new support: EZ only; the moon split is /84 here and the return of the sun is recalled with B3 S12.
  • Dropped U10.14.22 (EZ: by many narrators 5:55 restricts wilaya to God, His Messenger and Ali): no sound framing: Imamat ground, and 5:55 sits with S07 (AH P038, P052).
  • Dropped U10.14.24 (JS: the Itra is "the lesser weight" though the speaking Quran): no sound framing: off the step.
  • Dropped U10.14.25 (EZ: takwini and tashri'i wilaya, the father's limited legislative wilaya, 2:257): no sound new support: what wilaya is belongs to S07, and the father's limited wilaya is already EZ P081 (Ask 4).
  • Dropped U10.14.26 (EZ: the infallibles are addressed by 76:30 and 81:29): no sound new support: 76:30 of them is already in S06 (MM P010:2) and in EZ H7 P133:14 here.
  • Dropped U10.14.27 (EZ, JS: the covenant of restraint; Imam Rida did not pray for himself when poisoned; Ali could have prevailed; Karbala; miracles law-governed): no sound framing here: it is restraint, the subject of S09 (U9.14 D), not tafwid; EZ/JS only. It belongs with S09 if anywhere (Ask 19).
  • Dropped U10.14.28 (EZ: "I fashioned you for Myself", 20:41): no sound framing: how He builds His prophets, S07's servanthood step.
  • Dropped U10.14.29 (EZ, JS stories: al-Sadiq answering many tongues, al-Sajjad weeping for a destitute friend, Ali's restraint in wealth, the kites, and more): no sound new support: the al-Sajjad story is RR5-065 (recalled), the restraint stories are S09's subject, the rest are wonders (S07).
  • Dropped U10.14.34 (AH: receiving grace needs a receptive receiver; the perfect man must be in a body): no sound framing: U9.15.
  • Dropped U10.14.35 (AH: even the grace of Ali and the Imams reaches us only through the Imam of the Age): no sound framing: U9.15, and a doctrine the diagnosis flags for Ali (DIAGNOSIS problem 7).
  • Dropped U10.14.36 (AH: sacrificing the lower for the higher; the table spread for the honoured guest; Halabi's oath on refuge): no sound framing: U9.15 and U9.16.
  • Dropped U10.14.37 (AH: from the living effect know the living cause, the proof is alive): no sound framing: U9.15.

Texts and Jami results 7
  1. «وأجرى طاعتهم عليها وفوّض أمورها إليهم فهم يحلّون ما يشاؤون ويحرّمون ما يشاؤون ولن يشاؤوا إلا أن يشاء الله» (JS P077:2-3, Imam al-Jawad to Muhammad b. Sinan): verified (al-Kafi 1:441 via al-Wafi 3:682; texts no. 178); in full here, the Bab's first use.
  2. «سترنا عنكم أكثر مما أظهرنا لكم ... إن الله أقدرنا على ما نريد» (JS P087:3, al-Baqir to Jabir): verified, Mustadrak Safina 9:186, Riyad al-abrar 91 (texts no. 179, 189); trimmed.
  3. «فجعل لنا الفضيلة بالملكة على جميع خلقه فكل خليقته منقادة لنا بقدرته وصائرة إلى طاعتنا بعزته» (JS P093 to P094, H11 P229): FOUND-OTHER-WORDING, Sahifa du'a 1 (texts no. 104); one telling, Jami's «صائرة».
  4. «بخع كل متكبر لطاعتكم ... وذلّ كل شيء لكم» (EZ P088:2, JS P087:7): Ziyara Jami'a, al-Muhtadar 1:26 (texts no. 180); one clause.
  5. «فإنا صنائع ربنا والناس بعد صنائع لنا» / «نحن صنائع ربنا والخلق بعد صنائعنا» (JS P103, EZ P101): FOUND-OTHER-SPEAKER and OTHER-WORDING: the first is Ali's letter (Nahj, letter 28), the second the Imam of the Age's tawqi' (Bihar 53:178), not Ali's (texts no. 106 to 108).
  6. «أنت المالك وأنا المملوك» (EZ P099:23): FOUND-OTHER-WORDING, Amir al-Mu'minin's munajat at the Kufa mosque (texts no. 109).
  7. «إن الإمام وكر لإرادة الله عز وجل لا يشاء إلا ما شاء الله» (JS P118:5): found only in al-Muhtadar 1:227 (a late work), «ما شاء» not «ما يشاء» (texts no. 186); and «فبحق من ائتمنكم على سره واسترعاكم أمر خلقه وقرن طاعتكم بطاعته» (JS P106:37-41): closing of Ziyara Jami'a Kabira (texts no. 181). Also «قد أذن الله في فرجك» (JS P114:14): Saduq's Amali via Wasa'il 17 (texts no. 185); «بما نطق فيهم من مشيتك» (EZ H8 P171:7): verified, the Rajab du'a (al-Misbah 529).
Next lesson picks up

اگر ان کے پاس اللہ کی دی ہوئی ایسی قدرت ہے تو وہ اسے اپنے لیے، اپنے دشمنوں کے خلاف یا ظلم روکنے کے لیے کیوں استعمال نہیں کرتے، اور اتنی قدرت ان کے ہاتھ میں بگڑتی کیوں نہیں؟ (If they hold such God-given power, why do they not use it for themselves, against their enemies or to stop oppression, and why does such power not go wrong in their hands?) (S09)

S09 اولیائے خدا کی قدرت خطا سے محفوظ ہے، کیونکہ خدا اپنے چنے ہوئے واسطوں کو معصوم رکھتا ہے؛ اور ان کا کمال یہ ہے کہ ایسی قدرت رکھتے ہوئے اسے کبھی اپنے لیے کام میں نہیں لاتے

ledger · 8 new · 8 part-taught before · 0 taught before · 0 kept in full
The steps 8
  1. If they hold such power, what keeps it from going wrong? Error comes from one who acts by his own choice; when God chooses an intermediary He blocks the road of his error, so the messengers and the Imams are sinless (EZ H11 P238:8-17, P238:19-28 the axe likeness). Within the Bab, one line recalls S08 step C2 for H7 P133:2-16 (the absolute given only to the one kept from error, 76:30), which S08 carries. (/139) [/139 NEW]
  2. How far does that go? Every word and act of the Prophet shows God's will, even at home; the polished mirror of his being (EZ H11 P233:1-16, 53:3-4, P234:8-10). Anyone may claim inspiration, but satans inspire too (6:121); the guarantee of being kept is given to the Fourteen and the prophets only, not to the greatest jurist (P236:1-9, P236:16-21, P236:30-31; «لا تکلنا الی انفسنا» and 23:97 at P236:19-20). 33:33: «أذهب عنه» means God never lets the impurity in, not that He cleans it after (P237:5-11; 33:33 alone in the Handout layer, Kisa lines TC after a check). Parrot line, one line (P242:13-19). (/135, /137, /138, /141; RR1-006 TC) [/135 NEW | /137 NEW, TC only, ASK | /138 PART: recall B1 S14 "EZ on 33:33: rijs is doubt, God removed it from them, so they have certainty" then new: «أذهب عنه» as never letting impurity enter, the ground of their sinlessness (P237:5-11) | /141 PART: recall B7 S19 "Hafiz's parrot behind the mirror said of the Prophet as conveyor of God's words" then new: EZ's claim it fits only the Prophet absolutely, never us (P242:13-19), TC]
  3. Did they ever act against God's will? Never, neither His takwini nor His tashri'i will; Imam Hasan's peace was by His will and command; Hasan and Husayn in one direction, ten years of following with no difference in a single question (EZ H11 P239:5-9, P241:3-6, P241:14-15). (/140; /136 TC) [/140 PART: recall B7 S21 "Imam Hasan's peace is an example of a wise act whose wisdom was hidden from the objectors" (also B7 S23 plan, as a mercy) then new: EZ's general claim that they never acted against either will (P239:5-6) | /136 NEW, TC only, ASK]
  4. Then why do they not use the power for themselves? A covenant with God: Imam Musa al-Kazim sends his cellmate home and stays under the chain himself, "we have a covenant with God not to take personal benefit from what He gave us" (EZ H8 P178:7-34, P180:7); the Prophet closes the zakat to Banu Hashim when his house is hungry, and the condition of zuhd taken from them (P181:1-17); Imam Husayn at Ashura could have overturned the plain (P173:15-26); Solomon used the wind (38:36), the Imams were given the like and chose not to (P186:5-17). One line recalls S07 (U9.14/104, P187: they break nature only to prove the truth). (/102, /103; RR2-019 TC; import U11.31/2 TC) [/102 PART: recall B8 S16 "the Imams hold the Greatest Name beyond Asaf's one letter, so they can do more" then new: they never used it for themselves, the covenant (P178:24-34, P180:7) | /103 PART: recall B3 S12 "JS reads Solomon's kingdom and 38:35 so that it does not exclude the Prophet and the Imams" (and B8 S16, Asaf's one letter) then new: the Imams were given Solomon's powers and chose not to use them (P186:7-17), 38:36 once]
  5. How did the Prophet show it? Stoned and fallen, he is offered the service of the angels of the sun, the sea and the mountains to destroy his people; he answers "I am the prophet of mercy, not of punishment" and prays «اللهم اهد قومي فإنهم لا يعلمون» (AH H9 P208:1-13, P208:22-23, P208:29-34; 21:107). The AH carrier of the lesson. (/117) [/117 NEW]
  6. Is the use forbidden to them? No: the power is lawful and pure for them (P252:9-12); the "ban" is not haram, it is what was agreed (P181:18-22), "better not" (P262:40-44, P266:31-34); so it is never on demand: no one can promise the Imam will come and heal (AH RR2-063). (/148; RR2-063) [/148 PART: recall B3 S12 "AH: a God-given (wahbi) power acts in the world" (and B8 S16 step 3 and 4, power over creation by the Greatest Name) then new: EZ's claim that this power is lawful for them and they choose not to use it (P252:9-12 reconciled with P181:18-22)]
  7. Why then the dagger and the burnt door? They allowed it by a takwini permission, not a tashri'i one: they never say "I make it lawful", they say "you did wrong" (EZ H12 P261:9-19); forbearance belongs only to the one who has power: "let not Satan take your forbearance" to Zaynab (P257:1-12); Fatima's threatened curse and Ali's "we do not do this" (P257:13 to P258:11, ASK); Fidda's cup as the house's way (P259:7, P260, fine layer). (/153, /152; RR2-020 TC; import U11.31/1 TC) [/153 PART: recall B7 S15 "even a tyrant's wrong happens only by His takwini permission, which is not His pleasure" then new: the Imams themselves gave that takwini permission for the dagger and the broken door (P261:9-17) | /152 NEW, TC]
  8. Does power corrupt? No: it reveals the corrupt; God is the most powerful and not corrupt, nor is the Prophet or Ali at the summit of power (P262:1-6, P256:1-5); the minister's keys and the light switch (P262:13-39, P264:5-20, fine layer); the key of all being, the Greatest Name, is theirs and they leave it unused (P263:21-26, P264:1-4, P264:13-14); the strongest beings did not become taghut, they put the head in sajda and say "do not leave us to ourselves" (P266:9-26). Our side: humility before His friends (P253:12-14, TC). (/154, /155; /149 TC) [/154 NEW, images fine layer | /155 NEW | /149 PART: recall B1 S17 "JS: no one reaches divine knowledge except through humility before the Imams" then new: the Shi'i's dignity is in that humility, theirs in wanting no one's abasement (P253:12-14), TC, ASK]

Who carries it

EZ (steps 1 to 4, 6 to 8, about 85% of the lesson); AH carries step 5 only (H9 P208) and adds RR2-063 (step 6) and three TC records; MM absent. Imports 9
  • U11.31/2 (AH, AZ-S17 ¶73, ¶105-106; AZ-S19 ¶8-9): kept, step 4. Claude's judgement AH gives a second wisdom for the prophets and their successors mostly showing their outward side and holding back their inner power: if they showed a little more, people would call them gods; this supports the step "why the power is not used" beside EZ's covenant, as another reason for the same restraint.
  • U11.31/1 (AH, HLB_01_S21 ¶3503-3505): kept, step 7, only P002:3 and P003:1-2 (Ali overpowered and killed by Ibn Muljam, Husayn driven out and killed by Shimr, the Hujja fearful, "lest anyone believe their divinity"). Claude's judgement AH's line that God lets His greatest friends be overpowered and killed so that none takes them for gods gives one reason why they allow the dagger and the door, beside EZ's takwini permission. Not P001:2 «يجعله غير عالم» (the Prophet made "not knowing", waiting for revelation): that needs a doctrinal frame (ASK); the Tawqi' AH names is not verified.
  • U11.17/1 (AH, ZH-S23 ¶29-33; AZ-S23 ¶36-38): kept, step 2, P001:1-2 and P002:2 only. Claude's judgement AH's "Ali did not associate anything with God for a blink, from his mother's womb to the grave" is read as one instance of EZ's reading of 33:33, that the impurity (here shirk, the gravest) was never let in, not cleaned after. Not P001:3 or MV-S01 P003:2 (the other companions as idol worshippers: polemic, not this step). The du'a «بروح علي ... الذي ما أشرك بالله طرفة عين» is not verified.
  • U11.31/3 (AH, Uhud: not God, not a sorcerer): dropped, no sound framing: it is about the wisdom of the defeat, not about power held back, and its "not God" reason is already in U11.31/1 and /2.
  • U11.31/4 (AH, EZ; people shaken by wonders, Ali's eye pain): dropped, no sound framing beyond /2: the same principle told again, and the eye-pain story teaches the pain of a disbeliever's death, not restraint.
  • U11.31/5 (AH; the infant Jesus first says "I am the servant of God", 19:30, then is beaten and flees): dropped, no sound framing: it frames servanthood against deification of Jesus, not the Imams' restraint; the principle is already carried by /2.
  • U11.17/2 (AH, EZ; from the seed, the paradise pomegranate, to the last breath): dropped: the same claim as U11.17/1 in a story frame that adds nothing to step 2.
  • U11.17/3 (AH, EZ; a long past of idol worship leaves a trace, so the one who never did shirk should lead): dropped, no sound framing: an Imamat argument about who should lead, not ismah of power.
  • U11.17/4 (AH; the boy Ali breaks the idols, the voice from the womb): dropped: a childhood story of the same claim as U11.17/1, with no further support for the step.

Texts and Jami results

53:3-4 «وَمَا يَنطِقُ عَنِ الْهَوَىٰ» (H11 P233:12); 6:121 clause «وَإِنَّ الشَّيَاطِينَ لَيُوحُونَ إِلَىٰ أَوْلِيَائِهِمْ» (P236:6, already printed B1 S07); 33:33 «إِنَّمَا يُرِيدُ اللَّهُ لِيُذْهِبَ عَنكُمُ الرِّجْسَ» (P237:4, already printed B7 S16); 38:36 «فَسَخَّرْنَا لَهُ الرِّيحَ تَجْرِي بِأَمْرِهِ» (H8 P186:11); 21:107 with «اللهم اهد قومي فإنهم لا يعلمون» (H9 P208:32-33); «يا أخية لا يذهبن حلمك الشيطان» (H12 P257:1; Jami: al-Irshad 93-94, Bihar 45:2; printed «لا یذهبن به حلمک شیطان» adds «به», correct to Jami); «أعطانا الله ذلك كله بالاسم الأعظم» (H12 P264:2, Bihar 26:7, verified; speaker unnamed in the lesson). Next lesson picks up

کیا زمین ایک لمحے کے لیے بھی حجت سے خالی رہ سکتی ہے؟ (Can the earth be without a proof for a single moment?) (S10)

4. The mediator in creation and law, the proof as spirit, the refuge S10 to S12

S10 «زمین ایک لمحہ بھی حجت سے خالی نہیں، اور حجت اس عالم کی روح ہے»

ledger · 15 new · 11 part-taught before · 0 taught before · 1 kept in full
The steps 8
  1. Does reason require a proof of God on earth? Yes. The walled and watered garden shows the tree has fruit: so long as the order of the world stands and we are kept, the fruit, the proof, exists. A ship needs a captain. God who gave the fly and the woodlouse a governing spirit would not leave the great cosmos without one. So this world always needs a governing intellect, and against God's perfect godhood there must always be a servant perfect in servitude. (AH H5 P126:2-9, P127:1-3, P128:7-11, P140:1-4, P142:2-4, P143:1-2; H1 P005:4-8; U9.2-1 P017:4-6, P018:3-8, P021:7; points U9.15/49, /50, /57, /5, U9.2/13.) [/49 PART: recall B7 S22 "the Wise does nothing in vain, humans the trunk and the Proof the fruit" then new: the walled, watered garden, P126:2-9, P127:1-3 | /50 PART (changed from NONE): recall B1 S11 "a tent cannot stand without a pillar, a flock is not safe without a shepherd, without a guide we are far from the kind Creator" then new: the ship and its captain and AH's turning the images into questions about the world, P128:7-11 | /57 NEW | /5 NEW | U9.2/13 NEW]
  2. Why can the earth not be without him even for a moment? By the rule of the nobler possible (امکانِ اشرف) grace reaches the nobler first and through it the lower: no plant without the animal rank, no animal without man, no man without the perfect man. Skipping (طفرہ) is impossible, as a lamp lights the nearest space first. So while one human is on earth the proof must be. Said once, not three times. (AH H5 P134:2-7, P130:1-11, P131:1-2; H4 P122:2-11; H1 P006:2-7, P009:19-21, P010:9; points /53, /51, /6.) [/53 NEW | /51 PART: recall B3 S02 "light reaches the near space first" (also B7 S02, giving by degrees, Karun and jug) then new: the rule against skipping applied to God's lights reaching through the nearest, P130:1-11 | /6 CORE REPEAT kept in full: base "no age is left without an infallible Imam" (B1 S11; B7 S22) is the lesson's own claim; new: AH's argument "while one human is on earth there must be a perfect man", P006:2-7, P009:19-21, P010:9]
  3. Who is he? A man in our form who stands to us as we stand to animals: outwardly like us, inwardly of another rank («النَّاسُ مَعَادِنُ»); the perfect man of every age, today the Imam of the Age. (AH H1 P008:1-3, P008:9-10, P010:3-5, P009:1-4; virtues digression P008:5-7, P011 to TC; point /8; RR2-042.) [/8 PART: recall B7 S21 "the higher is not sacrificed for the lower, we were made for the prophets" (TC there) then new: the rank ratio, perfect man to us as man to animals, P008:1-3, P008:13-15 (one telling) | RR2-042 NEW]
  4. What must he have, and why must he be in a body? Divine knowledge, divine power and infallibility. And a body: giver and receiver must be in proportion; the sun gives full light, yet our weak eye needs dark glasses, and the world of matter is that glass. (AH H1 P007:1-4, P006:10-11; H5 P131:2, P132:4-48 with the lizard story in one sentence, P133:5-8; points /7, /52.) [/7 PART: recall B1 S11 "the earth always has an infallible proof" then new: AH's rational case that he must have divine knowledge, power and infallibility, P007:1-4 | /52 PART: recall B7 S02 "the weak receiver cannot take His full giving at once" then new: the sun and the dark glasses, the medium the Imam's body, P132:4-48, P133:5-8]
  5. What is he to the world? Its spirit and sustainer (قیوم), as soul to body; he is before the world and the world is made for him; the world was the body of the Prophet, then of Amir al-Mu'minin, one Imam after another, today of Baqiyyat Allah; as a magnet holds iron filings, his body holds the body of the cosmos (a simile, marked as one); he is its axis. (AH H1 P018:1-27; H2 P019:1-12 with 39:30 in one line; H5 P136:1-11, P138:5-8; points /12, /13, /54.) [/12 PART: recall B1 S11 "the world of being stands by the one who knows God" (MM) then new: the rational argument that the cosmos must have a spirit and qayyum (qayyum said so far only of God), P018:1-27 | /13 PART: recall B1 S11 (the world stands by them, they are the cause of creation) and B7 S21 TC ("we were made for the prophets") then new: the perfect man as the world's spirit, the world the body of one Imam after another, H2 P019:1-12 | /54 NEW]
  6. Do the texts say so? Al-Sadiq: «الْحُجَّةُ قَبْلَ الْخَلْقِ وَمَعَ الْخَلْقِ وَبَعْدَ الْخَلْقِ» (block), read by AH: before by rank, after as the final cause, with as soul with body; and Ziyarat Jami'a «وَأَسْمَاؤُكُمْ فِي الْأَسْمَاءِ ...», whose "in" is the "in" of sustaining, not of a vessel ("water in the jug"). (AH H1 P012:1-4, P013:3-6, P014:3-20, P015:19-28; H2 P020:17-20, P021:1-7, one telling; points /9, /10, /14; RR5-039.) [/9 NEW | /10 NEW | /14 NEW | RR5-039 PART: recall B1 S10 "13:7, I am the warner, Ali the guide" then new: the two proof narrations]
  7. And without him? Bodies would scatter into dust, stars and galaxies with them; the earth would sink with all its «أهل», people, animals, plants and minerals. The narration in Jami's wording, with AH's own note on the wording. (AH H1 P017:1-4; H5 P144:1-2, P144:11; point /11; RR5-016, RR5-017, RR4-017, RR3-043, RR1-017.) [/11 PART: recall B1 S11 (MM) and B3 S19 "without him no earth, every motion above and below is by him" (AH, also B4 S20) then new: the proof as spirit and qayyum of every body in his wilaya, the scattering of bodies and stars, P017:1-4]
  8. And the hidden Imam? Not idle: his being benefits creation in occultation as in presence (creative benefit; the legislative one named, never explained), and for himself too his being is not void. Close and hand-over: through whom does the command come down? (AH H5 P139:1, P137:1-4; U9.16-2 P018:1-5; points /55, U9.16/16; RR1-018, RR2-052, RR3-016, RR5-028, RR1-032, RR2-008.) [/55 NEW | U9.16/16 PART: recall B1 S10, S11 ("the hidden proof as the sun behind clouds") and B7 S21 TC ("the hidden wali is not futile") then new: the creative and legislative benefit, U9.16-2 P018]

Next lesson picks up

«امر کس پر اترتا ہے، اور شبِ قدر کس کی رات ہے؟» (On whom does the command descend, and whose night is the Night of Qadr?) (S11)

S11 خدا کا امر خاتم النبیین صلی اللہ علیہ و آلہ اور ان کے بعد ان کے وصی پر اترتا ہے، اور شبِ قدر اس کی سب سے بڑی دلیل ہے

ledger · 7 new · 6 part-taught before · 0 taught before · 0 kept in full
The steps 8
  1. What is the Seal to the system? Seal of what preceded and opener of what comes; he bears his Lord's knowledge of the system to the measure placed in him, so he is mediator in creation and in law, and the knowledge of prophets and angels about the system is from his; he owns the system first to last because his light is the first issued (MM H1 P001:1-2, P002:2, P003:3; U9.15/1, /2, /3). ↺ B1 S11 · B1 S09 · B1 S07Already taught: B1 S11 "the Imams are the first creation and the cause of creation, the first and the last; the world stands by one who bears God's knowledge to teach creation" (and B1 S09, AH: a prophet entrusted with teaching; B1 S07, AH: simple water owning the first light; the first-issued claim itself is taught in S06 of this Bab, recalled in one line)New here: the Seal as seal and opener, bearer of the system's knowledge to its measure, hence creative as well as legislative mediator and owner of the system, MM P001:1-2, P002:2, P003:3:1-2
  2. What is his work as mediator in law? To stir the light of the intellects and perfect them by reminding them of reason's verdicts, since human intellects are rays of his intellect and all knowing is by his light (MM U9.14 H9 P193:2-3, P194:2; U9.14/107). The re-read lines that prophets are needed because we cannot know God's pleasure or how to thank Him, and that the details of the law sit in an inner Quran carried by them, are recalls only (RR1-002, RR2-024, RR2-034, RR4-031, RR5-031, RR5-032, RR5-027). new
  3. Why him and not another? God knows best where to place His message (6:124): He knows whom to make the intermediary; and all He gave the prophets from Adam to Isa He gave the Seal and the Imams, and today it is with the Imam of the Age (JS H2 P028:6-9, P029:12-18; U9.15/16, /17). ↺ B1 S11 · B7 S10 · B8 S16Already taught: B1 S11 "JS's 6:124 as the Khariji's admission that a right word on God shows who deserves to guide" and B1 S11 "MM: the knowledge of Adam and the prophets is in the family of Muhammad" (also B7 S10: He chose His friends knowing their end; B8 S16: the Greatest Name, 72 letters)New here: 6:124 read as God's choice of the intermediary (P028:6-9), and the general claim that every prophet's gift was given to the Prophet and the Imams (P029:14-18)
  4. On whom does the command descend? On the one standing on the face of the earth: the Messenger of God, then his wasi after him; the command comes down to him from above the heavens (65:12 with al-Rida's reading; MM H4 P116:1-3; U9.15/46). Below him the chain in law runs to his deputies in the cities (AH RR5-063, ZH-S19 ¶71-74, Yunus and al-Amri). new
  5. Where is the proof of it? Laylat al-Qadr: each year the angels and the Spirit descend by their Lord's leave with every command (97:4), and in it every wise matter is decided (44:4); ask: on whom do they descend, God's khalifa and the governor of the system? On the Seal, then from Amir al-Mu'minin to the awaited Proof; so al-Baqir: argue with Inna anzalnahu and you will win, it is God's proof on creation after the Messenger (MM H1 P004:1-2, H4 P115:6-8; U9.15/4, /44). new
  6. What do they bring him? Every measure of the coming year (provision, life, power), written and set before the Imam of the time, who signs it, and God still effaces and confirms (13:39); seeing his Shia in hardship he has their provision increased, then signs (EZ, JS U9.14 H9 P188:3-4, P189:1-12, P190:1-6; EZ H12 P267:7-18; U9.14/105, U9.14/156). [NEW for /105; PART for /156: recall B3 S12 "AH: Imam Zayn al-Abidin weeping at a Shia's hardship and relieving it once God permitted" then new: EZ's Imam on the Night of Qadr increasing his Shia's provision before he signs, P267:16-18]
  7. Whose night is it, when nights differ on a turning earth? The night of the horizon where God's khalifa lives; he governs the system and the angels act by his leave for every hair, leaf and grain (MM H4 P115:19-21 with H1 P004:3 and P003:3; U9.15/45). new
  8. Why did he come down to us at all? Had We made him an angel We would have made him a man (6:9): the Messenger and the Imams accepted descent to the human rank and sitting with people to train them, among their greatest acts of worship and their humility before God; AH: the royal falcon of Lahut brought down among the Arabs to make them human, a hardship that raises his own degree (MM H4 P117:1-2; U9.15/47; AH RR1-020, EJ-S08 ¶51-67). new Close: the one on whom every command descends is the one through whom every grace reaches us (hand-over to S12).

فیض کو اس کے راستے سے کیوں گزرنا ہے، اس کے راستے سے ہم تک کیا پہنچتا ہے، اور ہم اس کی پناہ میں کیسے جائیں؟ (S12)

S12 خدا کا ہر فیض، روزی سے بارش اور رحمت تک، اس کے ولی کے راستے سے آتا ہے؛ خدا کے بعد اسی کے حکم سے وہی مخلوق کی پناہ ہیں، مگر وہ اس کے بندے ہیں، شریک نہیں

The steps 8
  1. Why should grace come through a channel at all, if God could give without one? God is not unable to create without His wali, but He set an order; the prophets have a face to God and a face to creation; grace reaches the nobler first, and the Imam takes from malakut and gives to mulk, to each by his measure; the wali is the hand that freely puts itself at His disposal. (JS U9.14-7 P127:1-11, EZ P128:1-27 with 67:30; EZ U9.14-7 P132:2-15; EZ U9.15-3 P089:6-12, P084; AH U9.16-1 P011:1, P012:1-3; points U9.14/80, /83, U9.15/33, U9.16/11; ground lines RR1-001, RR4-006, TC.) [NEW: U9.14/80, U9.15/33 | PART: U9.14/83 recall B1 S02 "the perfect servant is the smooth road on which God's will moves" then new: the wali as the channel of the creative will by his own choice (P132:12-15) | PART: U9.16/11 recall B3 S19 "by his blessing the whole world is fed; the table of being is spread for him" then new: how it is given, from malakut to mulk, nobler first (U9.16-1 P011:1, P012:1-3) | SAME: RR1-001, RR4-006 recall only]
  2. What reaches creation through him? Every grace: provision for all and the standing of earth and heaven; in this world God's hand is His friends and proofs, and the Imam of the Age is the hand through which He gives. (EZ U9.15-2 P026:6 on, P027; JS P031:9-24, P034:11-13, P035:10-11, EZ U9.15-3 P091:4-5, text #18 once as a dua line; AH U9.16-2 P017:3-5, P026:4-26; JS P030 TC; points U9.15/15, /19, /18, U9.16/15, /20; RR2-049, RR3-015, RR5-069.) [CORE REPEAT: U9.15/15 kept in full, flagged | PART: U9.15/19 recall B1 S11 "by us rain falls and the earth grows" then new: the 'Adila line applied to the Imam of the Age, JS P031:9-24 | PART: U9.16/15, /20 recall B1 S11 "His hand spread over them with mercy" then new: AH's "God's hand in this world is His friends and proofs" and "the Imam of the Age is the hand through which He gives" (U9.16-2 P017:5, P026:11, P026:19-26) | SAME: RR2-049, RR3-015, RR5-069 recall only]
  3. Which blessings, by the texts? Rain, fruit and mercy: "through us the trees bore fruit and the rain of heaven falls"; "by you God sends down the rain"; "through you, him and the Imams of his sons I have mercy on My servants". (JS U9.15-3 P082:8-11, P082:17-19, text #34 one block; EZ P083:5, text #32; JS P081:3-4, text #36; JS U9.15-6 P149 TC; JS P085 TC; points U9.15/29, /28, /61, /30; RR4-046.) ↺ B1 S11Already taught: U9.15/29 recall B1 S11 "by us rain falls from the sky" (Urdu line)New here: rain sent through them as a claim of its own, JS P082:8-11, EZ P083:5 | PART: U9.15/28 recall B4 S20 "the Mi'raj passage, there for the Qa'im filling the earth with tasbih" then new: the clause read for the Imams as the means of mercy, JS P081:3-4 | NEW: U9.15/61, /30 (TC) | SAME: RR4-046 recall only
  4. Is what they give their own? No: it is God's gift placed in their hands, like the rich father's child; power reaches creatures through a channel God measured, so one may say "O Hujjat of God, give". (EZ U9.15-3 P093:2-40, P094:17-21; EZ P091:1-5, P091:13-21, P092:1-3, text #27 in Jami's wording; points U9.15/36, /35.) [NEW: U9.15/36 | PART: U9.15/35 recall B7 S02 "His giving reaches the finite creature graded and measured (Karun and the jug)" then new: EZ's channel as the Imam and asking "O Hujjat of God, give", P091, P092]
  5. Who holds this channel today, and what is he to the world? The Baqiyyat Allah, the last of the chain; the proof, hidden or present, is the channel of grace; he is the link between creation and Creator that encompasses creation, the heart of the world (Hisham's debate) and the axis of existence. (JS U9.15-3 P079:1-9 with 11:86 as ta'wil, AH U9.15-2 P019:7; AH U9.16-2 P021:1-27, P022:6-8, P025:2-3; JS U9.15-2 P051 to P054; EZ U9.15-3 P090:1-29; EZ U9.15-2 P064:45-49, P065, JS P062; JS P046 to P049, EZ P050 TC; EZ U9.15-3 P088:1-16 TC; points U9.15/26, U9.16/19, U9.15/21, /34, /23, /20, /32; RR5-042, RR3-044, RR4-049, RR4-022, RR1-031 TC.) [NEW: U9.15/26, /34, /20, /32 | PART: U9.16/19 recall B1 S11 "the earth is never empty of a proof, manifest or hidden" then new: AH's "hidden or present, he is the channel of grace" (P021:14-27) | PART: U9.15/21 recall B1 S11 "you are My best creation between Me and My creation" then new: the Imam of the Age as the link encompassing creation, JS P052 to P054 | PART: U9.15/23 recall B1 S11 "creation stands by them" then new: the term "axis of existence", EZ P064:45-47, P065 | PART: RR5-042 | SAME: RR1-031 recall only]
  6. Who is our refuge, and from what? God first, then in His line the Messenger and the Imams: al-kahf al-hasin, the refuge of creation, in whom the earlier prophets too took refuge (the five shining nails of the Ark); refuge from Satan, the self and sins; and faith is not belief alone but entering that refuge. (EZ U9.15-4 P095:2-20 with 61:11, P096:7-27, P097:10-38, P098:12-15; EZ U9.15-2 P070:4-21, JS P069:3-29; AH U9.16-2 P017:3, P017:6-7; JS U9.15-3 P077:2-10, EZ P078:10-11; JS P080 TC; EZ U9.16 /46 with 54:13; points U9.15/37, /24, /25, /27, /40, /39, U9.16/46; RR2-051, RR3-023, RR4-027, RR3-022, RR4-026, RR2-055, RR5-006, RR2-010, RR4-012 TC, RR4-041 TC, RR1-039 TC.) ↺ B7 S11Already taught: U9.15/37 recall B7 S11 "refuge is first in God"New here: refuge after Him in His Messenger, EZ P095:2-20, P096:7-8 | NEW: U9.15/24, /25, /27, /40, /39, U9.16/46 | PART: RR2-051, RR4-041 | SAME: RR3-023, RR4-027, RR3-022, RR4-026, RR2-055, RR4-012 recall only
  7. Why Imam al-Rida above all, and must one be at his shrine? God chose him and pleases through him (the salawat); whoever turns to him is not disappointed; his presence is not bounded by place, so a greeting from afar reaches, and life and death are no bar to their command; his favour has no border, even the gazelle. (EZ U9.15-4 P105:7-12, P106:10-28, JS P099:8-15, P102:6-7, text #14; EZ P112:1-11, JS P108:4-7, P109:4-7, P110:13-14, text #36; EZ U9.15-6 P152:8-17, text #29; EZ P114:16-23, JS P113:1-7; points U9.15/41, /38, /42, /64, /43; RR2-025, RR1-040, RR2-056, RR5-021.) [NEW: U9.15/41, /42, /64, /43 | PART: U9.15/38 recall B7 S27 "AH's report that turning to al-Rida's shrine answers the need" then new: EZ's statement of it as a rule, P105:7-12, P106:10-28 | PART: RR2-025 | SAME: RR5-021 recall only]
  8. Then are they God's partners? No: Imam Hasan at the mosque door forwent retaliation in servanthood; place no imamzada, scholar or sayyid in their rank; close: on Friday afternoons the believer's heart is with the Imam's, and one says "ya Hujjat Allah", which hands over to how to ask (S13, S14). (EZ U9.15-6 P155:2-21, text #30; EZ P156:18-21, P158:3, text #31; EZ P154:1-3; EZ U9.15-3 P092:4-6; points U9.15/66, /67, /65, /35 close.) [NEW: U9.15/66, /67, /65]

Texts and Jami results

«بِنَا أَثْمَرَتِ الْأَشْجَارُ ... وَبِنَا يَنْزِلُ غَيْثُ السَّمَاءِ» (al-Kafi 1:192-193, Jami FOUND); «وَبِكَ وَبِهِ وَبِالْأَئِمَّةِ مِنْ وُلْدِهِ أَرْحَمُ عِبَادِي وَإِمَائِي» (Mi'raj hadith, al-Amali, Nawadir al-akhbar 1:118; key clause FOUND; reprint B4 S20); «بِكُمْ فَتَحَ اللَّهُ وَبِكُمْ يَخْتِمُ وَبِكُمْ يُنَزِّلُ الْغَيْثَ» (Ziyarat Jami'a, Bihar 99:131, FOUND); «اللَّهُمَّ صَلِّ عَلَى عَلِيِّ بْنِ مُوسَى الَّذِي ارْتَضَيْتَهُ وَرَضِيتَ بِهِ مَنْ شِئْتَ مِنْ خَلْقِكَ» (Zad al-ma'ad 1:312, al-Balad al-amin 1:425, FOUND); «الكهف الحصين ... ملجأ الهاربين ... عصمة المعتصمين» (Jami: Sha'ban noon salawat for Muhammad and his family, not al-Rida's titles) with «وكهف الورى» (Ziyarat Jami'a, FOUND); «السَّلَامُ عَلَيْكَ يَا أَمِينَ اللَّهِ فِي أَرْضِهِ وَحُجَّتَهُ» (Farhat al-Ghari 1:70); «بَقِيَّتُ اللَّهِ خَيْرٌ لَكُمْ» (11:86, as ta'wil); «لو بقيت الأرض بغير إمام لساخت» (al-Kafi; the printed «لساقت الارض و اهلها» is only a summary form, FOUND-OTHER-WORDING; #27, first printed in S10); «بيمنه رزق الورى وبوجوده ثبتت الأرض والسماء» (Du'a al-'Adila only, Zad al-ma'ad 1:423, not a Masum text; once, as dua).

5. The wasila and tawassul S13 to S14

S13 وسیلہ اللہ نے خود مقرر کیا ہے: آدم سے آج تک اس کا فیض اور بخشش انہی ہستیوں کے راستے سے آتی ہے، اور ان سے توسل توحید کے اندر ہے، اس کے خلاف نہیں

ledger · 9 new · 11 part-taught before · 0 taught before · 0 kept in full
The steps 6
  1. Who taught tawassul first? God Himself: He showed Adam the five, named them by names drawn from His own, and said «هؤلاء خيار خليقتي... بهم آخذ وبهم أعطي... فتوسل إلي بهم يا آدم... لا أرد بهم سائلا» (MM U9.16-1 P001:1, block trimmed to the carrying clauses, the derived names one line); the Quran orders «وابتغوا إليه الوسيلة» (5:35) and they say «نحن الوسيلة», the Prophet in his life, the Imams in theirs, the Imam of the Age in ours (AH U9.16-2 P027:1-2); the Prophet is God's beloved by essence, and God told us to take him as means and intercessor (AH P014:1, P015:1-2) (points U9.16/1, /21, /13; RR1-021 recalled). [NEW (U9.16/1, /21) | PART: recall B7 S13 "AH: the Prophet is God's beloved by essence" then new: God's order to seek means and intercession through him, in P014:1, P015:2 (U9.16/13) | SAME: RR1-021 recalled only]
  2. What did Adam do, and what did he receive? After the error he sought means by Muhammad and his family in prostration and was forgiven and made khalifa (MM U9.16-1 P002:8, block cut to «فلما قارف آدم الخطيئة... توسل إلى الله وهو ساجد بمحمد... فغفر الله له خطيئته وجعله الخليفة في أرضه»); the «کلمات» of 2:37 were their names, «وكنا الكلمات» (EZ U9.16-3 P055:5, the one Adam text with 2:37); the formula once, «يا حميد بحق محمد» with the five (AH U9.16-2 P028:3, P029:3-7); and God names the five «وسيلتك ووسيلة من أسعدت من خلقي» (MM U9.16-3 P061:6, cut to the first two sentences, with MM's own note that they are the means of all the blessed). JS's P053 and EZ's P057 to P058 tellings go to the Teacher Copy (points U9.16/2, /29, /22, /33, /31 Teacher Copy). [NEW (U9.16/2, /29, /22, /31 Teacher Copy) | PART: recall B8 S12 "MM's Seth scroll narration, another clause (His mercy outran His wrath); the scroll's base is B4 S02" then new: the Prophet and the lights around him as Adam's and the blessed's means, in P061:6 (U9.16/33)]
  3. Could God not give without them? He could: the need for a medium is no lack in God, the barrier is our weakness (AH U9.16-4 P065:1-2, 15-20, without the polemic P065:1, 4); so He gives by the door, the means and the rope He let down, «السبب المتصل بين الأرض والسماء» (AH P064:2-3); His mercy reaches His servants «وبك وبه وبالأئمة من ولده أرحم عبادي وإمائي» (MM U9.16-1 P005:7, one clause), and the Prophet at Badr: «يا رب إن تهلك هذه العصابة لم تعبد» (MM P006:3, one clause, Ask); so whoever thinks he reaches any rank, knowledge, perfection or salvation without their mediation has forgotten the covenant (MM P003:1) (points U9.16/37 base sentence, /36 base sentence, /5, /6, /3). [NEW (U9.16/37 and /36, the base sentences; their depth lines are Teacher Copy, see below) | PART: recall B4 S20 "JS: by the Qa'im God fills His earth with tasbih (the Mi'raj passage)" then new: MM reads the clause on mercy through the Prophet, Ali and the Imams for mediation (U9.16/5) | PART: recall B4 S05 "EZ: the Badr prayer, the Prophet's concern that worship stay" then new: MM reads the band as the means by which God is worshipped (U9.16/6) | PART: recall B1 S11, S12 "MM: without them God would not be known or worshipped" then new: no rank, knowledge, perfection or salvation without their mediation, in P003:1 (U9.16/3)]
  4. Is this against tawhid? No: using means in material things keeps us true monotheists because we follow the best order God set, and so does tawassul by the spiritual means (AH U9.16-2 P032:1); God made them means and doors of His mercy; they are nothing of themselves, all they have and can do is by His grace and leave, so there is no harm in asking through them (JS U9.16-3 P039:5-10). EZ's "what they give they took from God" (P060:8-11) is homed in S14 (U9.16/32); one line here only if Ali wants it, worded so that asking is never called worship (diag Q13) (points U9.16/23 first half, /26; RR2-006, RR2-046, RR5-046 recalled). ↺ B7 S27Already taught: B7 S27 "using means does not go against reliance on God (tie the camel)"New here: means as the best order God set, in P032:1 (U9.16/23) | PART: recall B1 S11 "they are God's door that leads to Him, not to themselves" then new: doors of His mercy, nothing of themselves, all by His leave, in P039:5-10 (U9.16/26) | SAME: RR2-006, RR2-046, RR5-046 recalled only
  5. Who is the means today? The Prophet is the first and greatest means for all people from Adam to the Day, «وسيلة الوسائل» and «سبب الأسباب» (AH U9.16-2 P031:1-2); then Amir al-Mu'minin and the Imams, each in his time (P027:1, P032:1 first ranks only, diag Q4); today the Imam of the Age, set by God as the medium between Him and us: whatever we have is from him, so good or sinner, hold his hem (AH P016:1); the one connected to the wali never runs dry, as Qanbar a spring joined to Ali's ocean (RR5-005) (points U9.16/23 second half, /14; RR5-005; RR1-012, RR4-018, RR4-021, RR5-033 recalled). ↺ Bab 1 to 7Already taught: U9.16/23 as in step 4, the means of means is its new part | NEW (U9.16/14; RR5-005, AZ-S23 ¶162, polemic left out) | SAME: RR1-012, RR4-018, RR4-021, RR5-033 recalled only
  6. Close: then must one always ask through them? Asking God directly is not forbidden, and He helps one who asks in earnest (JS U9.16-2 P033:12-19, P034:7-13); but the better way is to ask Him by the right of those He named, because their standing with Him is beyond our thought (JS P034:14-16), as Fatima bint Asad asked by the right of the child in her womb (EZ P035:30-32, 41-43, two sentences, Ask) (point U9.16/24). ↺ B1 S11 · B7 S27Already taught: B1 S11 "EZ: Ali calls God by the right of al-Mustafa, his cousin and Fatima, the Imams' way" (also B7 S27, S28 Teacher Copy formulas)New here: the teaching itself, ask God but by the right of His friends, in JS P033 to P034 and EZ P035:41-43

Who carries it

MM carries steps 1 to 3 (P001, P002, P003, P005, P006, P061); AH carries steps 1, 3, 4 and 5 (P014 to P016, P027 to P032, P064 to P065), so AH and MM carry the whole ground. EZ adds the one Adam text with 2:37 (P055:5) and Fatima bint Asad at the close; JS carries step 4's "nothing of themselves" (P039) and step 6's "asking directly is not forbidden, the better way is through them" (P033 to P034). The AH re-read records here are almost all recalls (8 of 10 SAME): AH's new teaching in the lesson comes from the Fasl itself, not from the re-read; only RR5-005 (Qanbar) is new. Nothing borrowed to fill it.

Texts and Jami results

«هؤلاء خيار خليقتي... فتوسل إلي بهم يا آدم... لا أرد بهم سائلا» (God to Adam; Bihar 11:151, 26:327-328; the carrying clause in Mustadrak Safina 10:304, Ghayat al-Maram 178-179); «فلما قارف آدم الخطيئة... توسل إلى الله وهو ساجد بمحمد وحامته وأهل بيته فغفر الله له» (Bihar 26:314-315, Mustadrak al-Wasa'il 4:484 from Tafdil al-A'imma, marfu', a late source; the inline clause gave no hit); «نحن الوسيلة» with 5:35 (Jami: the Prophet, «نحن الوسيلة إلى الله والوصلة إلى رضوان الله», Mustadrak Safina 10:304; AH says it of the Imams); «فلما عصى آدم ربه سأله بحقنا... وكنا الكلمات» with 2:37 (Bihar 35:23, Rawdat al-Wa'izin 205); «يا حميد بحق محمد يا عالي بحق علي يا فاطر بحق فاطمة...» (FOUND-OTHER-WORDING: Bihar 44:245, 109:85, from al-Durr al-Thamin, Jami ends «يا محسن بحق الحسن والحسين ومنك الإحسان»; AH's «ويا قديم الإحسان بحق الحسين» is not in this text); «يا آدم هؤلاء وسيلتك ووسيلة من أسعدت من خلقي» (Bihar 26:312-313, 21:311); «وبك وبه وبالأئمة من ولده أرحم عبادي وإمائي» (Amali of al-Saduq, Bihar 23:128).

Next lesson picks up

S14 asks «توسل کیسے کیا جائے، کس چیز کے لیے، اور اس کی حد کیا ہے؟» (How is tawassul done, for what, and where is its limit?)

S14 اللہ سے محمد و آل محمد کے حق کا واسطہ دے کر مانگو، دنیا کی حاجت بھی اور نفس کا ہر کمال بھی؛ گناہ ان کی رضا سے بخشے جاتے ہیں مگر لوگوں کا حق باقی رہتا ہے، اور اس گھر کے در سے کوئی ناامید نہیں لوٹتا

ledger H14 · 17 new · 7 part-taught before · 0 taught before · 0 kept in full
The steps 8
  1. How does one ask? Ask God by their right: «اللهم إني أسألك بحق محمد وعلي فإن لهما عندك شأنا من الشأن وقدرا من القدر», because on the Day angels, prophets and tested believers all need the two of them (al-Kazim, MM H1 P004:3); God has bound Himself to forgive the servant who asks by the right of Muhammad and his family (MM H3 P062:5-6, the carrying clause from Jami); and one may ask the Imam himself, not only "intercede for me": asking al-Rida only to carry small needs to God is like begging a rich man's son to fetch ten tomans from his father, who gives him thousands (EZ U9.15-6 P157:3-4 claim, P157:34-45 the al-Rida dialogue, P157:7-33 image cut to one line, «يا وجيها عند الله اشفع لنا عند الله» once); AH: he is near, kind and listening, do not let Satan whisper "I am a sinner, he will not answer" (RR4-024), and the address to the Imam behind the cloud of occultation, by his mother Fatima, to ask God to untie the Shia's knots (RR4-045). Teacher Copy, fine layer: the Imams' order "when you call God, call Him by means of us" (JS H3 P037:5-6) and the ziyara to Amir al-Mu'minin «إنا توجهنا واستشفعنا وتوسلنا بك إلى الله ... يا وجيها عند الله اشفع لنا عند الله» (EZ H3 P038:3-5). (U9.16/4, /34, /25; U9.15/68.) [U9.16/4 NEW | U9.16/34 NEW (doubt read, see Repeats) | U9.15/68 NEW, one telling: S12 tells EZ's rich father's child (U9.15 H3 P093) for "their gift is God's", one recall line here, then the new use, "so ask the Imam himself" | U9.16/25 PART, Teacher Copy: recall B7 S28 "the formula «اللهم إني أتوجه إليك بنبيك» (JS, fine layer)" then new: the Imams' order to call God by means of them, JS P037:5-6, and the Dua Tawassul formula, EZ P038:4-5 | RR4-024 PART, RR4-045 PART | RR1-003, RR1-016, RR1-044, RR3-024, RR3-035, RR3-036, RR4-025, RR5-043 SAME: recall only]
  2. Did the infallibles themselves ask so? Yes: Amir al-Mu'minin «اللهم إني أتوسل إليك في يوم فقري وفاقتي ... بحبك وبحبيبك» (JS H4 P072:4-6); Ibn Mas'ud found Ali in prayer asking by the right of Muhammad for the sinners of his Shia, and the Prophet asking by the right of Ali for the sinners of his community, «فلا أعلم أيكما أوجه عند الله من الآخر» (JS H4 P073:19-23, 33, 37-38, 54; the scene in prose); and Ibrahim in the fire had already asked «اللهم إني أسألك بحق محمد وآل محمد لما نجيتني منه», so he could send Jibra'il back; his "not from you" never meant asking without means (EZ H4 P079:16-24, P080:2-5, the minister analogy P079:5-15 neutralised or out). (U9.16/42, /43, /45.) [U9.16/42 PART: recall B1 S11 "Ali asks God by the right of al-Mustafa (EZ, his munajat)" then new: the du'a «بحبك وبحبيبك», JS P072:4-6 | U9.16/43 PART: same B1 S11 base, then new: Ibn Mas'ud's report, each by the other, JS P073:19-23, 33, 54 | U9.16/45 NEW]
  3. What does one ask for, and from whom? Worldly needs, and also every perfection and the change of the self: the self is rebellious and hard to face alone, so say in earnest "Master, I am stuck where I was, one favour so that I move and change"; one glance of theirs does the work, since the Imam of the Age is informed, able by God's leave, and kind (JS H3 P046:3-6, P050:1-18; JS U9.14-7 P126:4-12 one clause, the glance of Imam Hasan or Imam Husayn that sets a person right); the Imam may cast most good deeds into the heart of one who turns to him, unseen (AH H2 P020:1-7); a lover who clings to their hem with a true wish is given what he asks (RR2-033); the student joined to the Imam as a spring to the ocean does not dry up (RR1-029); with more love of him veils lift (RR1-009). Whom: the merchant sends small buyers to his younger son's shop so that the shop runs, so small needs are often sent down the ranks; after their martyrdom al-'Abbas and Ali al-Asghar were made gates of needs so the road of tawassul stays open; al-Kazim was Bab al-Hawa'ij in his life; the seminaries sit beside the shrines so that students keep up tawassul (EZ U9.15-6 P164:1-30, P165:1-4, 14-23, P166:1-20, reconciled with P157 in one line). (U9.16/27, /28, /18; U9.14/79; U9.15/70.) [all NEW | RR1-009 PART | RR2-033, RR1-029 NEW | RR1-013 NONE, Teacher Copy (Ibn Tawus: our love of the Imam is self-love) | RR1-030, RR3-037 SAME: recall only]
  4. Does tawassul protect? None escapes the devils' traps but who holds to them, «فلن ينجو منها إلا من تشبث بنا ... وعباده أولياؤنا» (Ali to Kumayl, MM H1 P007:1, with «واذكرنا وسم بأسمائنا» and «واستعذ بالله وبنا» inline, P007:3); remember Muhammad and his family in hardship, and keep up obedience, dhikr and the salawat on them (MM H1 P008:2-3 first sentence, P008:12-15); «فنحن عروة الله الوثقى، من استمسك بنا نجا» (MM H1 P009:7, one clause); and the elders whose whisperings left after tawassul to the infallibles, the strongest being tawassul to Imam Husayn (JS H3 P037:7-9, P056:3-7). (U9.16/7, /8, /9, /30.) [U9.16/7 NEW | U9.16/8 PART: recall B8 S08 "God's name keeps the devils away (MM, angels strike them)" then new: the salawat on Muhammad and his family repels them, MM P008:2, P008:15 | U9.16/9 PART: recall B4 S02 "the firmest handle is Ali's wilaya (one line)" then new: «فنحن عروة الله الوثقى» as their mediation, MM P009:7 | U9.16/30 NEW]
  5. And sins, and the Day? «إذا كان يوم القيامة نشفع في المذنب من شيعتنا» (MM H1 P004:1); some sins only their pleasure removes, «يا ولي الله إن بيني وبين الله عز وجل ذنوبا لا يأتي عليها إلا رضاكم» (JS H3 P059:11-16, EZ H3 P060:14-17; the U9.14-a H6 P106 copy joins here, one place); the Prophet is Abu al-Qasim because he distributes God's mercy among His servants on the Day (MM H3 P063, one clause «إنما كنيتك أبا القاسم لأنك تقسم الرحمة مني بين عبادي يوم القيامة»); Husayn was an intercessor from the day of his birth: the angel Fitrus touched his cradle and was given back his wing (EZ U9.2-4 P108:7-10, 22-39, short; the al-Jawad sequel and tabarruk at the zarih P108:13-20, 40-45 Teacher Copy); AH: "a man is with whom he loves", so do not let go of love of the family of Muhammad (RR5-030), and no fear of the Gathering's sun under Ali's shade (RR4-019, one line of verse). (U9.16/32, /35; U9.2/46; /4 second clause.) [all NEW | RR5-030 PART | RR4-019 NEW | RR3-038 SAME: recall only]
  6. Where does mediation stop? Ali intercedes, and God forgives His own right through him; the rights of people (honour, wealth, life) must still be settled; so leave the world with sound beliefs and settle what is owed (JS H4 P070:11-13, 16, P071:16-19, 30-33 for the claim; the story P070:2-10, 19-21, P071:2-12 is JS's own retelling, Teacher Copy until a narration is found). (U9.16/41.) [U9.16/41 PART: recall B7 S26 "people's rights remain and must be settled even when God forgives" then new: Ali's intercession does not cancel them, JS P070:11-13, P071:30-33]
  7. Then why does tawassul often bring no result? So does du'a to God: He too does not give all we ask; it has its own reckoning and conditions, and what is not good for us is not given; yet many times they have given, and given much; asking God directly is no fault, nor is taking His friends as means (JS H4 P067:6-16, H3 P046:8-12, P077:20-27, one telling). (U9.16/39.) [U9.16/39 PART: recall B7 S28 step 1 "He answers every call but does not do every wish; a withheld prayer has its reason (EZ)" (also B8 S18, Mujib, plan) then new: the parity reply to the objection against tawassul, JS P067:9-16 | RR2-022 SAME: recall only]
  8. Close: is this door ever shut? Bind yourself to those complete in the love of God, who bore everything for His religion; say to them "you are our refuge, whom else shall we tell?" (JS H4 P068:2-15, P069:4-9); none returns hopeless from this house: one story (JS H4 P074:3-14, the shut-out child let in when an elder knocks, recommended; or P075, the youth at al-'Abbas's gaze); AH: love is two-sided, one step for the Imam and he takes ten (RR1-043); though we are unclean his grace reaches us and he never leaves us empty-handed (RR2-053, without the comparison with the eight Imams ¶116); grasp his hem, the best way (RR1-041); his hem is let down for anyone who serves the faith (RR3-046); love him and his kindness is tenfold (RR2-011); al-Saduq's difficulty solved when he began the book on the occultation (RR5-044); Ibn Battuta's witness of the sick healed in Ali's shrine on 27 Rajab (RR5-007); and the du'a for the hastening of the reappearance. Teacher Copy, fine layer: the Ghadir poem, "every hard knot of creation is open in your knot-loosening hand" (EZ U9.15-6 P167:1-12, one line). (U9.16/40, /44; U9.15/71.) [U9.16/40, /44 NEW | U9.15/71 NEW, Teacher Copy | RR1-041, RR3-046, RR2-011 PART | RR1-043, RR2-053, RR5-044, RR5-007 NEW | RR1-028, RR2-054, RR5-045 SAME: recall only | RR2-071, RR4-028, RR4-030 Teacher Copy, off-subject]

  1. U9.15/68 and /70 (asking the Imam himself; the ranks, Bab al-Hawa'ij, the seminaries) taught here with EZ P157 and P164 reconciled in one line: "the Imam can give all; small needs are often sent down the ranks so that every door stays open"? Recommended yes (U9.15-b Q17, Q26; step 1, 3).

  • PART, AH re-read: RR2-011 (EJ-S10 ¶151-153): base "Recall B4 S15: the Imam loves the Shia of Iran for keeping the rites of Husayn and Fatima (AH)"; new: love him and, by his own self, his kindness becomes tenfold (step 8).
  • PART, AH re-read: RR3-046 (ZH-S29 ¶70-75): base "Recall B3 S04 (also B6 S01, B7 S28): the hidden Imam watches over his Shia and helps unseen (AH)"; new: whoever serves the faith sincerely gets his wondrous help; we are lazy, yet his hem is let down for anyone to grasp (step 8). Within the Bab S13 teaches holding his hem (U9.16/14): one recall clause.
  • PART, AH re-read: RR4-024 (EJ-S13 ¶33-40): base "Recall B1 S08: the Imam watches over the Shia like a father; speak to him openly (AH)"; new: Satan's whisper "I am a sinner, he will not answer" is false; he is near, kind, listening, kinder than a father (step 1).
  • PART, AH re-read: RR4-028 (EJ-S13 ¶45-54, Teacher Copy): base "Recall B4 S15 (also B5 S07, B6 S07): pray for the Imam of the Age in prayer (AH)" (B8 S18 RR8-040: pray in your own tongue); new: acts that breed love of him and draw his special care, the qunut du'a «اللهم كن لوليك» (step 8, off-subject, Teacher Copy).
  • PART, AH re-read: RR4-045 (ZH-S28 ¶184-190): base "Recall B7 S27 (Teacher Copy as written): swear the Imam by Sayyida Fatima so that he asks God for relief (AH)"; new: the address itself, the sun behind the cloud who knows the pain of hearts (step 1).
  • PART, AH re-read: RR5-030 (MJ-S03 ¶110-123): base "Recall B7 S25 (Teacher Copy as written): love of Al Muhammad kept after sin brings sincere repentance or intercession at death (AH)"; new: «المرء مع من أحب», with Imam Husayn by loving him (step 5). Sensitive as in B7 (it may read as licence to sin): keep with the repentance clause.

Texts and Jami results 7
  1. «اللَّهُمَّ إِنِّي أَسْأَلُكَ بِحَقِّ مُحَمَّدٍ وَعَلِيٍّ فَإِنَّ لَهُمَا عِنْدَكَ شَأْناً مِنَ الشَّأْنِ وَقَدْراً مِنَ الْقَدْرِ ...» (al-Kazim to Sama'a; MM H1 P004:3): FOUND, al-Kafi 2:529, al-Da'awat of al-Rawandi.
  2. «... وَلَكِنَّهُ حَتْمٌ عَلَى نَفْسِي أَنْ لَا يَسْأَلَنِي عَبْدٌ بِحَقِّ مُحَمَّدٍ وَأَهْلِ بَيْتِهِ إِلَّا غَفَرْتُ لَهُ» (al-Baqir; MM H3 P062:5-6): FOUND, al-Amali of al-Tusi no. 1425; MM's «لأظلت» is a misprint for «لأطلت»; the old verified=no flag is wrong.
  3. Dua al-Tawassul: «إِنَّا تَوَجَّهْنَا وَاسْتَشْفَعْنَا وَتَوَسَّلْنَا بِكَ إِلَى اللَّهِ ... يَا وَجِيهاً عِنْدَ اللَّهِ اشْفَعْ لَنَا عِنْدَ اللَّهِ» (EZ H3 P038:4-5, U9.15-6 P157:34): FOUND, Tuhfat al-Za'ir 1:643, Bihar 99:247-249; EZ's address to Amir al-Mu'minin is right; printed once in the Bab, here.
  4. «اللَّهُمَّ إِنِّي أَتَوَسَّلُ إِلَيْكَ فِي يَوْمِ فَقْرِي وَفَاقَتِي ... بِحُبِّكَ وَبِحَبِيبِكَ» (JS H4 P072:4): FOUND-OTHER-SPEAKER, Bihar 92:358-359 names no Imam; and «اللَّهُمَّ بِحَقِّ عَلِيٍّ وَلِيِّكَ إِلَّا مَا غَفَرْتَ لِلْمُذْنِبِينَ مِنْ أُمَّتِي» (JS H4 P073:33, with Ali's «بحق محمد نبيك ... من شيعتي» inline): FOUND, Bihar 36:73-74, Ta'wil al-Ayat 1:610-612, chainless.
  5. «... فَلَنْ يَنْجُوَ مِنْهَا إِلَّا مَنْ تَشَبَّثَ بِنَا ... وَعِبَادُهُ أَوْلِيَاؤُنَا» (Ali to Kumayl; MM H1 P007:1): FOUND, Bisharat al-Mustafa 1:28 (excerpt «تثبت»; Mustadrak Safina «تشبث» as MM); with «أَلَا فَاذْكُرُوا يَا أُمَّةَ مُحَمَّدٍ مُحَمَّداً وَآلَهُ عِنْدَ نَوَائِبِكُمْ وَشَدَائِدِكُمْ ...» (MM H1 P008:2, first sentence): FOUND, Bihar 91:12-13, from the contested Tafsir al-Askari; «فنحن عروة الله الوثقى، من استمسك بنا نجا» inline (MM P009:7): Tafsir Furat 1:552, Jami ends «هوى إلى النار».
  6. «يَا وَلِيَّ اللَّهِ إِنَّ بَيْنِي وَبَيْنَ اللَّهِ عَزَّ وَجَلَّ ذُنُوباً لَا يَأْتِي عَلَيْهَا إِلَّا رِضَاكُمْ ...» (JS H3 P059:15-16, EZ P060:16): FOUND, Tuhfat al-Za'ir 1:587, al-Muhtadar 1:220; with «إِذَا كَانَ يَوْمُ الْقِيَامَةِ نَشْفَعُ فِي الْمُذْنِبِ مِنْ شِيعَتِنَا» (MM H1 P004:1): FOUND, Bihar 8:59 from Fada'il al-Shi'a; and «إِنَّمَا كَنَّيْتُكَ أَبَا الْقَاسِمِ لِأَنَّكَ تَقْسِمُ الرَّحْمَةَ مِنِّي بَيْنَ عِبَادِي يَوْمَ الْقِيَامَةِ» (MM H3 P063, one clause): FOUND, Mustadrak Safina 2:393, al-Muhtadar 1:262 (the printed Bihar 18:315 excerpt stops before it).
  7. Fitrus: «فَمَرَّ بِجَزِيرَةٍ فِيهَا مَلَكٌ» and «قُلْ يَتَمَسَّحُ بِهَذَا الْمَوْلُودِ» (EZ U9.2-4 P108:25, 37): FOUND, al-Khara'ij 254-255, Bihar 44:182-183. Ibrahim's «اللهم إني أسألك بحق محمد وآل محمد لما نجيتني منه» (EZ P079:19-20, inline) has no texts-check row: to verify before print.

How this draft was made

What this draft does (the rules applied)
  • SAME: one recall line naming the earlier lesson; the scholar's repeat sits under it in the Teacher Copy as an Ask.
  • PART: the shared base recalled in one line, the new part taught in the scholar's own new wording.
  • Core repeat: kept in full where the repeat is the lesson's own claim, flagged as an Ask (8 in the Bab).
  • A Bab 7 or Bab 8 line that only recalls Bab 1 to 6 is not counted as Bab 7 or 8 teaching: the earliest lesson is named.
  • Arabic printed in full in an earlier Bab is printed again in the Teacher Copy, flagged with the earlier lesson.
  • Imports only in thin lessons, only from later Babs, Teacher Copy only, each with Claude's judgement line.
  • Nothing is cut on Claude's word: held points and off-subject records stay in the Teacher Copy, flagged.
